# 阿聖練等幫手 enUS 聯盟路線說明

- 只收聯盟路線獨有的說明列，共 **386** 列。

檔名:行號｜類型｜中文原文｜enUS
01-第一段-1到12級.md:22｜說明｜右鍵背包裡的被啃過的書接任務。｜Right-click Nibbled-On Book in your bags to start the quest.
01-第一段-1到12級.md:58｜說明｜向瑞雷‧派特斯金納學剝皮。｜Learn Skinning from Riley Peltskinner.
01-第一段-1到12級.md:67｜說明｜獎勵選剝皮卡，就算不打算練剝皮也先選，之後可放棄專業。｜Choose the Skinning card as the reward even if you don't plan to skin. You can drop the profession later.
01-第一段-1到12級.md:11｜說明｜人類出生地。別族要來，從閃金鎮沿大路往北進北郡山谷，找修道院前的維里副隊長。｜Human starting area. Other races: follow the road north from Goldshire into Northshire Valley and find Deputy Willem in front of the abbey.
01-第一段-1到12級.md:95｜說明｜在閃金鎮旅店綁爐石（43.80, 65.80）。｜Set your hearthstone at the inn in Goldshire (43.80, 65.80).
01-第一段-1到12級.md:102｜說明｜沿路打野豬，收 4 塊大塊野豬肉。｜Kill boars along the way for 4 Chunk of Boar Meat.
01-第一段-1到12級.md:103｜說明｜接下來會經過狗頭人礦坑 4 次，每次順手打 2–3 隻收任務物品。｜You'll pass the kobold mine 4 times. Kill 2–3 kobolds each time for the quest items.
01-第一段-1到12級.md:120｜說明｜故意死亡，在靈魂醫者復活，省下跑回去的時間。｜Die on purpose and resurrect at the Spirit Healer to save the run back.
01-第一段-1到12級.md:125｜說明｜沿路跑到這個點。｜Follow the road to this spot.
01-第一段-1到12級.md:128｜說明｜學附魔：艾爾文森林有一個任務要用，做完可放棄。｜Learn Enchanting: one quest in Elwynn Forest needs it. You can drop it afterwards.
01-第一段-1到12級.md:134｜說明｜向蜜雪兒·貝利學急救。｜Learn First Aid from Michelle Belle.
01-第一段-1到12級.md:144｜說明｜在附近營火故意燒死，到靈魂醫者復活，省跑路。｜Burn to death in a nearby campfire on purpose and resurrect at the Spirit Healer to save running.
01-第一段-1到12級.md:174｜說明｜多數職業可用跳柵欄反覆拉打的方式單刷，但很難，打不過就跳過。｜Most classes can solo this by jumping over the fence and kiting, but it's hard. Skip it if you can't win.
01-第一段-1到12級.md:176｜說明｜右鍵背包裡的呱奇的頭顱接任務。；精英魚人掉頭顱開任務，打得過才做。｜Right-click Croaky's Head in your bags to start the quest. The elite murloc drops the head that starts the quest. Only do it if you can win the fight.
01-第一段-1到12級.md:176｜說明｜右鍵背包裡的呱奇的頭顱接任務。｜Right-click Croaky's Head in your bags to start the quest.
01-第一段-1到12級.md:176｜說明｜精英魚人掉頭顱開任務，打得過才做。｜The elite murloc drops the head that starts the quest. Only do it if you can win the fight.
01-第一段-1到12級.md:178｜說明｜探索地點拿經驗。｜Explore the area for experience.
01-第一段-1到12級.md:179｜說明｜被蜘蛛打死後在赤脊山復活，省下跑路時間。｜Let the spiders kill you, then resurrect in Redridge Mountains to save travel time.
01-第一段-1到12級.md:181｜說明｜湖畔鎮｜Lakeshire
01-第一段-1到12級.md:209｜說明｜在暴風城旅店綁爐石（60.50, 75.20）。｜Set your hearthstone at the inn in Stormwind (60.50, 75.20).
01-第一段-1到12級.md:212｜說明｜到暴風城矮人區搭礦道地鐵到鐵爐堡。｜Go to the Dwarven District in Stormwind and take the Deeprun Tram to Ironforge.
01-第一段-1到12級.md:213｜說明｜鐵爐堡｜Ironforge
01-第一段-1到12級.md:257｜說明｜向布莉姬‧風暴撕裂者學剝皮。｜Learn Skinning from Brighid Stormflayer.
01-第一段-1到12級.md:219｜說明｜矮人與地精出生地。別族要來，從卡拉諾斯往西南穿過山洞隧道進谷。｜Dwarf and Gnome starting area. Other races: from Kharanos, head southwest through the tunnel into the valley.
01-第一段-1到12級.md:279｜說明｜穿過隧道後，往旅店的路上每隻野豬都打。｜After the tunnel, kill every boar on the way to the inn.
01-第一段-1到12級.md:288｜說明｜在卡拉諾斯旅店綁爐石（47.40, 52.50）。｜Set your hearthstone at the inn in Kharanos (47.40, 52.50).
01-第一段-1到12級.md:297｜說明｜優先完成「貝爾丁的補給」，沿路見到熊和野豬就打。｜Finish Stocking Jetsteam first. Kill any bears and boars you see on the way.
01-第一段-1到12級.md:308｜說明｜打野豬收峭壁野豬肋排 ×6。｜Kill boars for 6 × Crag Boar Rib.
01-第一段-1到12級.md:316｜說明｜不必殺雪人：等牠離開洞穴（最多約 5 分鐘），進去開箱就跑。｜No need to kill the yeti: wait for it to leave the cave (up to about 5 minutes), then go in, open the chest and run.
01-第一段-1到12級.md:323｜說明｜樓下有矮人守著酒桶，把雷霆麥酒交給他；他不在就直接和酒桶互動。｜A dwarf guards the keg downstairs. Give him the Thunder Ale. If he isn't there, interact with the keg directly.
01-第一段-1到12級.md:323｜說明｜樓下有矮人守著酒桶，把雷霆麥酒交給他｜A dwarf guards the keg downstairs. Give him the Thunder Ale.
01-第一段-1到12級.md:323｜說明｜他不在就直接和酒桶互動。｜If he isn't there, interact with the keg directly.
01-第一段-1到12級.md:387｜說明｜右鍵背包裡的基塞伊斯之牙接任務。｜Right-click Fang of Githyiss in your bags to start the quest.
01-第一段-1到12級.md:342｜說明｜夜精靈出生地。別族要來，從奧伯丁搭船到魯瑟蘭村，走傳送門進達納蘇斯，再沿路往東經多蘭納爾到幽影谷。｜Night Elf starting area. Other races: take the boat from Auberdine to Rut'theran Village, use the portal to Darnassus, then follow the road east through Dolanaar to Shadowglen.
01-第一段-1到12級.md:408｜說明｜沿路打蜘蛛，之後要 7 個小蜘蛛腿。｜Kill spiders along the way. You'll need 7 Small Spider Leg later.
01-第一段-1到12級.md:423｜說明｜在多蘭納爾旅店綁爐石（55.60, 59.80）。｜Set your hearthstone at the inn in Dolanaar (55.60, 59.80).
01-第一段-1到12級.md:445｜說明｜向札瑞恩學烹飪。｜Learn Cooking from Zarrin.
01-第一段-1到12級.md:452｜說明｜向拜恩希學急救。｜Learn First Aid from Byancie.
01-第一段-1到12級.md:505｜說明｜獵人：馴服喜歡的寵物，推薦野豬（衝鋒開怪快）。｜Hunters: tame any pet you like. A boar is recommended (Charge pulls fast).
01-第一段-1到12級.md:538｜說明｜走進達納蘇斯的傳送門到魯瑟蘭村。｜Take the portal in Darnassus to Rut'theran Village.
01-第一段-1到12級.md:548｜說明｜暴風城｜Stormwind
01-第一段-1到12級.md:571｜說明｜朝女王跳過去時使用天裔種族技能。｜Use your Skyborne racial while jumping toward the Cirrusfly Queen.
01-第一段-1到12級.md:593｜說明｜接下來幾個任務沿途收集風石簇，值得稍微繞路。｜Collect Windstone Clusters during the next few quests. They're worth a small detour.
01-第一段-1到12級.md:608｜說明｜到任務地點再使用薩普塔。｜Use the Earth Sapta only once you reach the quest location.
01-第一段-1到12級.md:557｜說明｜天裔精靈出生地，聯盟與部落共用島上的起始任務，離島時才分陣營。｜Skyborne starting area. Alliance and Horde share the island's starting quests and only split by faction when leaving the island.
01-第一段-1到12級.md:642｜說明｜在微風島旅店綁爐石（43.00, 43.20）。｜Set your hearthstone at the inn on Zephras Isle (43.00, 43.20).
01-第一段-1到12級.md:689｜說明｜在微風島旅店綁爐石（62.20, 72.60）。｜Set your hearthstone at the inn on Zephras Isle (62.20, 72.60).
01-第一段-1到12級.md:804｜說明｜右鍵背包裡的撕破的信函接任務。｜Right-click Ripped Missive in your bags to start the quest.
01-第一段-1到12級.md:859｜說明｜右鍵背包裡的黑鐵地圖接任務。｜Right-click Dark Iron Map in your bags to start the quest.
01-第一段-1到12級.md:874｜說明｜慢慢往下跳到前方平台，再落到地面。｜Carefully drop down to the ledge ahead, then down to the ground.
01-第一段-1到12級.md:876｜說明｜塞爾薩瑪｜Thelsamar
01-第一段-1到12級.md:897｜說明｜在塞爾薩瑪旅店綁爐石（35.40, 48.40）。｜Set your hearthstone at the inn in Thelsamar (35.40, 48.40).
01-第一段-1到12級.md:904｜說明｜限量商品，有貨才買。｜Limited stock. Buy only if it's available.
01-第一段-1到12級.md:916｜說明｜在鐵爐堡搭礦道地鐵到暴風城。｜Take the Deeprun Tram from Ironforge to Stormwind.
01-第一段-1到12級.md:847｜說明｜人類從暴風城搭礦道地鐵到鐵爐堡；夜精靈搭船到暴風城再轉地鐵。天裔精靈不走這站，直接從微風島搭船到黑海岸。｜Humans: take the Deeprun Tram from Stormwind to Ironforge. Night Elves: take the boat to Stormwind, then the tram. Skyborne skip this stop and sail straight from Zephras Isle to Darkshore.
02-12到30級.md:13｜說明｜奧伯丁｜Auberdine
02-12到30級.md:54｜說明｜往南多打狂暴薊熊，再到 38, 60 一帶找，打到 6–10 隻先停。｜Head south and kill more Rabid Thistle Bears, also around 38, 60. Stop after 6–10.
02-12到30級.md:61｜說明｜到附近的月井把水管裝滿。｜Fill the Empty Water Tube at the nearby moonwell.
02-12到30級.md:89｜說明｜哨兵嶺｜Sentinel Hill
02-12到30級.md:94｜說明｜調整視角可從外面直接拾取。｜Adjust your camera to loot it from outside.
02-12到30級.md:110｜說明｜專程繞路，附近沒有其他目標；趕路可跳過。｜This is a dedicated detour with no other objectives nearby. Skip it if you're in a hurry.
02-12到30級.md:110｜說明｜專程繞路，附近沒有其他目標｜This is a dedicated detour with no other objectives nearby.
02-12到30級.md:110｜說明｜趕路可跳過。｜Skip it if you're in a hurry.
02-12到30級.md:120｜說明｜右鍵背包裡的聖潔之書接任務。｜Right-click Tome of Divinity in your bags to start the quest.
02-12到30級.md:125｜說明｜亞麻布不到 10 塊就去拍賣場買，通常很便宜。｜If you have fewer than 10 Linen Cloth, buy some at the Auction House. It's usually cheap.
05-副本.md:24｜說明｜右鍵背包裡的Treaty of Understanding接任務。；尾王約 35% 血會恐懼，先清小怪，再把王拉回前一個房間打。；接任務處旁有拉桿，拉下開門就能直線跑出去。｜Right-click Treaty of Understanding in your bags to start the quest. The last boss fears at about 35% health. Clear the adds first, then pull the boss back into the previous room. There's a lever next to the quest giver. Pull it to open the door and run straight out.
05-副本.md:24｜說明｜右鍵背包裡的Treaty of Understanding接任務。；尾王約 35% 血會恐懼，先清小怪，再把王拉回前一個房間打。｜Right-click Treaty of Understanding in your bags to start the quest. The last boss fears at about 35% health. Clear the adds first, then pull the boss back into the previous room.
05-副本.md:24｜說明｜右鍵背包裡的Treaty of Understanding接任務。｜Right-click Treaty of Understanding in your bags to start the quest.
05-副本.md:24｜說明｜尾王約 35% 血會恐懼，先清小怪，再把王拉回前一個房間打。；接任務處旁有拉桿，拉下開門就能直線跑出去。｜The last boss fears at about 35% health. Clear the adds first, then pull the boss back into the previous room. There's a lever next to the quest giver. Pull it to open the door and run straight out.
05-副本.md:24｜說明｜尾王約 35% 血會恐懼，先清小怪，再把王拉回前一個房間打。｜The last boss fears at about 35% health. Clear the adds first, then pull the boss back into the previous room.
05-副本.md:24｜說明｜接任務處旁有拉桿，拉下開門就能直線跑出去。｜There's a lever next to the quest giver. Pull it to open the door and run straight out.
05-副本.md:9｜說明｜走到丹莫洛與洛克莫丹時先接進本前的任務，進本前不必專程回頭。｜Pick up the pre-dungeon quests when you reach Dun Morogh and Loch Modan; no need for a special trip back before the dungeon.
02-12到30級.md:208｜說明｜接下來沿路見到小月夜猛虎和狂暴薊熊就打。｜From here on, kill any Moonstalker Runts and Rabid Thistle Bears you see.
02-12到30級.md:229｜說明｜到月井把空的淨化碗裝滿。｜Fill the Empty Cleansing Bowl at the moonwell.
02-12到30級.md:236｜說明｜靠近營火才會觸發事件，現在打或晚點路線再經過時打都行。｜The event only triggers near the campfire. Do it now or when the route passes by again later.
02-12到30級.md:241｜說明｜往下一個任務途中，看到月夜雄虎就打（幼崽和族母會一起拉）。｜On the way to the next quest, kill any Moonstalker Sire you see (the runts and matriarch will come with it).
02-12到30級.md:245｜說明｜走到 39.2, 86.9，使用安努給的小瓶放在地上。｜Go to 39.2, 86.9 and use the vial Onu gave you to place it on the ground.
02-12到30級.md:251｜說明｜護送任務：組隊時等大家進度一致再接，並關掉自動接交。｜Escort quest: in a group, wait until everyone is at the same step before accepting, and turn off auto-accept.
02-12到30級.md:253｜說明｜右鍵背包裡的《深淵之神》接任務。；坑裡的怪低機率掉《深淵之神》，有掉才接。｜Right-click Book: The Powers Below in your bags to start the quest. Mobs in the pit have a low chance to drop Book: The Powers Below. Only accept it if it drops.
02-12到30級.md:253｜說明｜右鍵背包裡的《深淵之神》接任務。｜Right-click Book: The Powers Below in your bags to start the quest.
02-12到30級.md:253｜說明｜坑裡的怪低機率掉《深淵之神》，有掉才接。｜Mobs in the pit have a low chance to drop Book: The Powers Below. Only accept it if it drops.
02-12到30級.md:254｜說明｜護送任務：組隊時等大家進度一致再接，並關掉自動接交。；讓勘察員雷姆塔維爾先跑，只打先攻擊他的怪。｜Escort quest: in a group, wait until everyone is at the same step before accepting, and turn off auto-accept. Let Prospector Remtravel run ahead, and only fight mobs that attack him first.
02-12到30級.md:254｜說明｜讓勘察員雷姆塔維爾先跑，只打先攻擊他的怪。｜Let Prospector Remtravel run ahead, and only fight mobs that attack him first.
02-12到30級.md:262｜說明｜會來好幾波魚人，每波 2–3 隻；每波最後一隻拉離營火再殺，吃喝完再拉下一波，或找隊伍。｜Several waves of murlocs come, 2–3 per wave. Pull the last murloc of each wave away from the campfire before killing it, eat and drink, then pull the next wave. Or find a group.
02-12到30級.md:262｜說明｜會來好幾波魚人，每波 2–3 隻｜Several waves of murlocs come, 2–3 per wave.
02-12到30級.md:262｜說明｜每波最後一隻拉離營火再殺，吃喝完再拉下一波，或找隊伍。｜Pull the last murloc of each wave away from the campfire before killing it, eat and drink, then pull the next wave. Or find a group.

05-副本.md:41｜說明｜右鍵背包裡的染血徽記接任務。｜Right-click Bloodied Insignia in your bags to start the quest.
05-副本.md:46｜說明｜右鍵背包裡的羅德隆紋章接任務。｜Right-click Crest of Lordaeron in your bags to start the quest.
05-副本.md:47｜說明｜右鍵背包裡的Blood-Stained Parchment接任務。｜Right-click Blood-Stained Parchment in your bags to start the quest.
02-12到30級.md:328｜說明｜右鍵背包裡的未寄出的信接任務。｜Right-click An Unsent Letter in your bags to start the quest.
05-副本.md:72｜說明｜前兩件在副本門前的礦洞裡完成。｜Do the first two in the mine in front of the dungeon entrance.
05-副本.md:74｜說明｜尾王掉落開任務的物品，拾取後右鍵接。｜The last boss drops a quest-starting item. Loot it and right-click it to accept.
05-副本.md:60｜說明｜走到西部荒野時先接進本前的任務，進本前不必專程回頭。｜Pick up the pre-dungeon quests when you reach Westfall; no need for a special trip back before the dungeon.

02-12到30級.md:406｜說明｜阿斯特蘭納｜Astranaar

02-12到30級.md:443｜說明｜限量商品，有貨且還沒有才買。｜Limited stock. Buy only if it's available and you don't have one yet.
02-12到30級.md:444｜說明｜觀星任務線要有青銅管才能開始；沒有就先做別的部分，之後去拍賣場買。｜The stargazing quest line needs a Bronze Tube to start. If you don't have one, do the other parts first and buy it at the Auction House later.

02-12到30級.md:444｜說明｜沒有就先做別的部分，之後去拍賣場買。｜If you don't have one, do the other parts first and buy it at the Auction House later.
02-12到30級.md:445｜說明｜右鍵背包裡的梅力克的弓接任務。｜Right-click Merrick's Bow in your bags to start the quest.
02-12到30級.md:460｜說明｜粘糊的蜘蛛腿留著別賣，後面要交。｜Keep your Gooey Spider Legs; don't sell them. You'll turn them in later.
02-12到30級.md:487｜說明｜上樓拾取箱子。｜Go upstairs and loot the chest.

02-12到30級.md:540｜說明｜下面三件精英任務單人很難，找不到隊伍就全部跳過。｜The next three elite quests are hard solo. Skip them all if you can't find a group.
02-12到30級.md:595｜說明｜穿過山洞進石爪山脈。｜Go through the cave into Stonetalon Mountains.

05-副本.md:121｜說明｜走到黑海岸・第二次時先接進本前的任務，進本前不必專程回頭。｜Pick up the pre-dungeon quests when you reach Darkshore (2nd visit); no need for a special trip back before the dungeon.
05-副本.md:119｜說明｜梣谷 1440 14.50, 14.20｜Ashenvale 1440 14.50, 14.20
02-12到30級.md:621｜說明｜米奈希爾港｜Menethil Harbor
02-12到30級.md:638｜說明｜之後沿路打小濕地鱷魚收皮。｜From now on, kill Young Wetlands Crocolisks along the way for their skins.
02-12到30級.md:674｜說明｜下樓與屍體互動，附近有 1–3 隻精英；用寵物、冰霜新星或恐懼甩開，互動完照原路跑上來，別戀戰。｜Go downstairs and interact with the corpse. There are 1–3 elites nearby. Shake them off with a pet, Frost Nova or Fear. After interacting, run back up the way you came. Don't stay to fight.
02-12到30級.md:674｜說明｜下樓與屍體互動，附近有 1–3 隻精英｜Go downstairs and interact with the corpse. There are 1–3 elites nearby.
02-12到30級.md:674｜說明｜用寵物、冰霜新星或恐懼甩開，互動完照原路跑上來，別戀戰。｜Shake them off with a pet, Frost Nova or Fear. After interacting, run back up the way you came. Don't stay to fight.
02-12到30級.md:678｜說明｜從橋上跳下，到矮人旁拾取信件。｜Jump off the bridge and loot the letter next to the dwarf.
02-12到30級.md:679｜說明｜右鍵背包裡的浸水的信封接任務。｜Right-click Waterlogged Envelope in your bags to start the quest.

03-30到45級.md:264｜說明｜從山洞進去拿書，容易多拉怪，稀有怪在時更難。｜Enter the cave to get the book. It's easy to pull extra mobs, and harder when the rare is up.
03-30到45級.md:283｜說明｜塔倫迪斯營地｜Talrendis Point

03-30到45級.md:359｜說明｜留意巡邏的被遺忘者信差。｜Watch out for the patrolling Forsaken Courier.
05-副本.md:197｜說明｜走到濕地・第二次時先接進本前的任務，進本前不必專程回頭。｜Pick up the pre-dungeon quests when you reach Wetlands (2nd visit); no need for a special trip back before the dungeon.
05-副本.md:195｜說明｜提里斯法林地 1420 82.60, 33.80｜Tirisfal Glades 1420 82.60, 33.80

03-30到45級.md:426｜說明｜在塞拉摩旅店綁爐石（66.60, 45.20）。｜Set your hearthstone at the inn in Theramore (66.60, 45.20).
03-30到45級.md:481｜說明｜在藏寶海灣旅店綁爐石（27.00, 77.00）。｜Set your hearthstone at the inn in Booty Bay (27.00, 77.00).
03-30到45級.md:523｜說明｜之後見到禿鷲和狼都打，任務要用。｜From now on, kill any buzzards and wolves you see. A quest needs them.
03-30到45級.md:544｜說明｜留意巡邏的索葛蘭。｜Watch out for the patrolling Boss Tho'grun.
03-30到45級.md:548｜說明｜近戰較難，必要時晚點再做。｜This is harder for melee. Come back later if needed.
03-30到45級.md:565｜說明｜在鐵爐堡旅店綁爐石（18.60, 51.60）。｜Set your hearthstone at the inn in Ironforge (18.60, 51.60).
05-副本.md:227｜說明｜右鍵背包裡的破碎的項鏈接任務。｜Right-click Shattered Necklace in your bags to start the quest.
05-副本.md:228｜說明｜奧達曼一路打到格瑞姆洛克。｜Clear Uldaman all the way to Grimlok.
05-副本.md:243｜說明｜還沒拿到破碎的項鏈就進本打穴居人到掉為止，再出來。｜If you don't have the Shattered Necklace yet, go into the dungeon and kill troggs until it drops, then come out.
05-副本.md:209｜說明｜荒蕪之地 1418 44.60, 12.10｜Badlands 1418 44.60, 12.10

03-30到45級.md:599｜說明｜右鍵背包裡的諾博魯的木棒接任務。｜Right-click Noboru's Cudgel in your bags to start the quest.
03-30到45級.md:599｜說明｜打木棒諾博魯拿到灰色的棍棒，右鍵開任務。｜Kill Noboru the Cudgel for a gray cudgel and right-click it to start the quest.
03-30到45級.md:616｜說明｜在藏寶海灣旅店綁爐石（27.00, 77.20）。｜Set your hearthstone at the inn in Booty Bay (27.00, 77.20).
03-30到45級.md:659｜說明｜沿海灘留意瓶子，撿到可開任務。｜Look for bottles along the beach. Picking one up starts a quest.
03-30到45級.md:669｜說明｜缺的荊棘谷書頁去拍賣場補：1、4、6、8、10、11、14、16、18、20、21、24、25、26、27。｜Buy any missing pages of The Green Hills of Stranglethorn at the Auction House: 1, 4, 6, 8, 10, 11, 14, 16, 18, 20, 21, 24, 25, 26, 27.
03-30到45級.md:671｜說明｜三艘船的下層可能刷出科泰羅的謎語，拿到就右鍵接。｜Cortello's Riddle can spawn on the lower deck of any of the three ships. Right-click it to accept once you have it.
03-30到45級.md:707｜說明｜等瑞茲爾·銅栓離開建築物再動手。｜Wait until Rizzle Brassbolts leaves the building before you act.
05-副本.md:263｜說明｜走到荊棘谷・第三次時先接進本前的任務，進本前不必專程回頭。｜Pick up the pre-dungeon quests when you reach Stranglethorn Vale (3rd visit); no need for a special trip back before the dungeon.

03-30到45級.md:792｜說明｜右鍵背包裡的OOX-22/FE定位器接任務。｜Right-click OOX-22/FE Distress Beacon in your bags to start the quest.
04-45到60級.md:49｜說明｜右鍵背包裡的OOX-17/TN定位器接任務。｜Right-click OOX-17/TN Distress Beacon in your bags to start the quest.
04-45到60級.md:93｜說明｜在鷹巢山旅店綁爐石（13.80, 41.60）。｜Set your hearthstone at the inn in Aerie Peak (13.80, 41.60).
04-45到60級.md:105｜說明｜右鍵背包裡的OOX-09/HL定位器接任務。｜Right-click OOX-09/HL Distress Beacon in your bags to start the quest.
04-45到60級.md:126｜說明｜沿途通常會掉熾熱火焰附魔；沒掉就放棄。｜You usually get Fiery Blaze Enchantments along the way. If none drop, abandon it.
04-45到60級.md:126｜說明｜沿途通常會掉熾熱火焰附魔｜You usually get Fiery Blaze Enchantments along the way.
04-45到60級.md:126｜說明｜沒掉就放棄。｜If none drop, abandon it.
04-45到60級.md:132｜說明｜擊殺狂暴的瑪爾戈，經驗很多。｜Kill Margol the Rager for a lot of experience.
04-45到60級.md:133｜說明｜右鍵背包裡的瑪爾戈的角接任務。｜Right-click Margol's Horn in your bags to start the quest.
04-45到60級.md:203｜說明｜跳進岩漿故意死亡：有個任務只能在靈魂狀態接。｜Jump into the lava to die on purpose: one quest can only be accepted as a ghost.
04-45到60級.md:204｜說明｜跑回屍體復活。｜Run back to your corpse and resurrect.
04-45到60級.md:205｜說明｜在黑石深淵練。｜Level in Blackrock Depths.
04-45到60級.md:214｜說明｜右鍵背包裡的科泰羅的謎題接任務。；50 級才能接。｜Right-click Cortello's Riddle in your bags to start the quest. Requires level 50 to accept.
04-45到60級.md:214｜說明｜右鍵背包裡的科泰羅的謎題接任務。｜Right-click Cortello's Riddle in your bags to start the quest.
04-45到60級.md:214｜說明｜50 級才能接。｜Requires level 50 to accept.
05-副本.md:283｜說明｜灼熱峽谷 1427 34.80, 85.30｜Searing Gorge 1427 34.80, 85.30
04-45到60級.md:235｜說明｜右鍵背包裡的仔細折疊的便箋接任務。；之前拿到的折好的紙條現在右鍵開任務。｜Right-click Carefully Folded Note in your bags to start the quest. Right-click the folded note you got earlier to start the quest now.
04-45到60級.md:235｜說明｜右鍵背包裡的仔細折疊的便箋接任務。｜Right-click Carefully Folded Note in your bags to start the quest.
04-45到60級.md:235｜說明｜之前拿到的折好的紙條現在右鍵開任務。｜Right-click the folded note you got earlier to start the quest now.
04-45到60級.md:285｜說明｜收集紅、藍、綠、黃水晶各 7 個，並從迅猛龍身上拿殘破的日誌。｜Collect 7 each of red, blue, green and yellow crystals, and get A Mangled Journal from the raptors.
04-45到60級.md:289｜說明｜在 67.7, 30.7 一帶打植物，練到 49 級一半。｜Kill plants around 67.7, 30.7 until you're halfway through level 49.
04-45到60級.md:295｜說明｜這件較難，可留到回安戈洛時再做。｜This one is harder. You can save it for when you return to Un'Goro.
04-45到60級.md:322｜說明｜把手上的擊殺、收集任務做完。｜Finish the kill and collection quests you have.
04-45到60級.md:351｜說明｜在阿斯特蘭納旅店綁爐石（37.00, 49.20）。｜Set your hearthstone at the inn in Astranaar (37.00, 49.20).
04-45到60級.md:377｜說明｜右鍵背包裡的薩瓦里克長笛接任務。｜Right-click Flute of Xavaric in your bags to start the quest.
04-45到60級.md:380｜說明｜刺枝林地｜Talonbranch Glade
04-45到60級.md:406｜說明｜後面可以跳上去抄捷徑。｜Further on, you can jump up to take a shortcut.
04-45到60級.md:408｜說明｜使用背包裡的法杖。｜Use the staff in your bags.
04-45到60級.md:413｜說明｜打雪人時有掉原始雪人毛皮就右鍵接。｜If a Pristine Yeti Hide drops while killing yetis, right-click it to accept.
04-45到60級.md:443｜說明｜喝下維德雷藥劑後保持死亡狀態，以靈魂找任務 NPC。｜After drinking the Videre Elixir, stay dead and find the quest NPC as a ghost.
04-45到60級.md:446｜說明｜回屍體復活。｜Return to your corpse and resurrect.
04-45到60級.md:488｜說明｜在奧伯丁旅店綁爐石（37.00, 40.00）。｜Set your hearthstone at the inn in Auberdine (37.00, 40.00).
04-45到60級.md:512｜說明｜右鍵背包裡的空的火酒瓶接任務。｜Right-click Empty Firewater Flask in your bags to start the quest.
04-45到60級.md:760｜說明｜還沒到 60 級就把冬泉谷任務做完，或打一趟黑石深淵；還不夠再刷斯坦索姆或通靈學院。｜If you're not 60 yet, finish the Winterspring quests or run Blackrock Depths once. If that's still not enough, run Stratholme or Scholomance.

06-路線外任務.md:9｜說明｜千針石林・第一次（第29站）經過時，有空再接。｜Pick this up if you have time when you pass through Thousand Needles (1st visit, stop 29).

06-路線外任務.md:17｜說明｜塔納利斯・第一次（第38站）經過時，有空再接。｜Pick this up if you have time when you pass through Tanaris (1st visit, stop 38).

06-路線外任務.md:21｜說明｜路線經過該區時等級落差大，跳過不影響練級。｜Your level is far off when the route passes this zone. Skipping it won't hurt your leveling.
06-路線外任務.md:25｜說明｜路線不經過這區，跳過不影響練級。｜The route doesn't go through this zone. Skipping it won't hurt your leveling.
06-路線外任務.md:135｜說明｜寒脊山谷（第3站）經過時，有空再接。｜Pick this up if you have time when you pass through Coldridge Valley (stop 3).

06-路線外任務.md:233｜說明｜費伍德森林・第一次（第47站）經過時，有空再接。｜Pick this up if you have time when you pass through Felwood (1st visit, stop 47).

06-路線外任務.md:461｜說明｜梣谷・第二次（第20站）經過時，有空再接。｜Pick this up if you have time when you pass through Ashenvale (2nd visit, stop 20).
06-路線外任務.md:657｜說明｜安戈洛環形山・第二次（第50站）經過時，有空再接。｜Pick this up if you have time when you pass through Un'Goro Crater (2nd visit, stop 50).
06-路線外任務.md:759｜說明｜北郡山谷（第1站）經過時，有空再接。｜Pick this up if you have time when you pass through Northshire Valley (stop 1).
06-路線外任務.md:764｜說明｜艾爾文森林・閃金鎮（第2站）經過時，有空再接。｜Pick this up if you have time when you pass through Elwynn Forest – Goldshire (stop 2).
06-路線外任務.md:779｜說明｜西部荒野（第11站）經過時，有空再接。｜Pick this up if you have time when you pass through Westfall (stop 11).
06-路線外任務.md:867｜說明｜赤脊山・第一次（第14站）經過時，有空再接。｜Pick this up if you have time when you pass through Redridge Mountains (1st visit, stop 14).

06-路線外任務.md:1600｜說明｜專業任務，有練這項專業再做。｜Profession quest. Only do it if you have this profession.
06-路線外任務.md:1688｜說明｜梣谷・第一次（第16站）經過時，有空再接。｜Pick this up if you have time when you pass through Ashenvale (1st visit, stop 16).
06-路線外任務.md:1709｜說明｜梣谷・第三次（第28站）經過時，有空再接。｜Pick this up if you have time when you pass through Ashenvale (3rd visit, stop 28).
06-路線外任務.md:1716｜說明｜是路線任務「盎格庫」的前置，先補做才接得到。｜Prerequisite for the route quest "Ongeku". Do it first to unlock that quest.
06-路線外任務.md:1729｜說明｜淒涼之地（第36站）經過時，有空再接。｜Pick this up if you have time when you pass through Desolace (stop 36).

06-路線外任務.md:1866｜說明｜菲拉斯・第二次（第48站）經過時，有空再接。｜Pick this up if you have time when you pass through Feralas (2nd visit, stop 48).

06-路線外任務.md:2025｜說明｜黑海岸・第二次（第13站）經過時，有空再接。｜Pick this up if you have time when you pass through Darkshore (2nd visit, stop 13).
06-路線外任務.md:2045｜說明｜千針石林・第二次（第31站）經過時，有空再接。｜Pick this up if you have time when you pass through Thousand Needles (2nd visit, stop 31).
06-路線外任務.md:2051｜說明｜泰達希爾（第40站）經過時，有空再接。｜Pick this up if you have time when you pass through Teldrassil (stop 40).
06-路線外任務.md:2060｜說明｜費伍德森林・第二次（第49站）經過時，有空再接。｜Pick this up if you have time when you pass through Felwood (2nd visit, stop 49).
06-路線外任務.md:2190｜說明｜微風島・出生地（第7站）經過時，有空再接。｜Pick this up if you have time when you pass through Zephras Isle – starting area (stop 7).
06-路線外任務.md:2192｜說明｜微風島・聯盟線（第8站）經過時，有空再接。｜Pick this up if you have time when you pass through Zephras Isle – Alliance route (stop 8).
06-路線外任務.md:2194｜說明｜是路線任務「犯罪元素」的前置，先補做才接得到。｜Prerequisite for the route quest "The Criminal Element". Do it first to unlock that quest.
06-路線外任務.md:2196｜說明｜是路線任務「女士之淚」的前置，先補做才接得到。｜Prerequisite for the route quest "Tears of the Lady". Do it first to unlock that quest.
06-路線外任務.md:2203｜說明｜節慶或特殊分類任務，依活動開放再做，不影響練級。｜Holiday or special-category quest. Do it when the event is active; it doesn't affect leveling.
06-路線外任務.md:2556｜說明｜安戈洛環形山・第一次（第46站）經過時，有空再接。｜Pick this up if you have time when you pass through Un'Goro Crater (1st visit, stop 46).
06-路線外任務.md:2558｜說明｜是路線任務「奎恩提斯的請求」的前置，先補做才接得到。｜Prerequisite for the route quest "Jonespyre's Request". Do it first to unlock that quest.
06-路線外任務.md:2586｜說明｜塔納利斯・第二次（第41站）經過時，有空再接。｜Pick this up if you have time when you pass through Tanaris (2nd visit, stop 41).
06-路線外任務.md:2639｜說明｜暮色森林・第二次（第22站）經過時，有空再接。｜Pick this up if you have time when you pass through Duskwood (2nd visit, stop 22).
06-路線外任務.md:2670｜說明｜是路線任務「失蹤的使節」的前置，先補做才接得到。｜Prerequisite for the route quest "The Missing Diplomat". Do it first to unlock that quest.
06-路線外任務.md:2678｜說明｜暴風城・第二次（第25站）經過時，有空再接。｜Pick this up if you have time when you pass through Stormwind (2nd visit, stop 25).
06-路線外任務.md:2679｜說明｜荊棘谷・第二次（第33站）經過時，有空再接。｜Pick this up if you have time when you pass through Stranglethorn Vale (2nd visit, stop 33).
06-路線外任務.md:2686｜說明｜燃燒平原（第52站）經過時，有空再接。｜Pick this up if you have time when you pass through Burning Steppes (stop 52).
06-路線外任務.md:2741｜說明｜是路線任務「烈焰精華」的前置，先補做才接得到。｜Prerequisite for the route quest "A Taste of Flame". Do it first to unlock that quest.
06-路線外任務.md:2783｜說明｜濕地・第二次（第23站）經過時，有空再接。｜Pick this up if you have time when you pass through Wetlands (2nd visit, stop 23).
06-路線外任務.md:2791｜說明｜是路線任務「可疑的蹄印」的前置，先補做才接得到。｜Prerequisite for the route quest "Suspicious Hoofprints". Do it first to unlock that quest.
06-路線外任務.md:2797｜說明｜職業任務，到接取點附近或回主城訓練時順手處理。｜Class quest. Handle it when you're near the quest giver or back in a capital for training.
06-路線外任務.md:2819｜說明｜是路線任務「艾魯拉的陰影」的前置，先補做才接得到。｜Prerequisite for the route quest "The Shade of Elura". Do it first to unlock that quest.
06-路線外任務.md:2856｜說明｜是路線任務「身心之力」的前置，先補做才接得到。｜Prerequisite for the route quest "Body and Heart". Do it first to unlock that quest.
06-路線外任務.md:3202｜說明｜冬泉谷（第51站）經過時，有空再接。｜Pick this up if you have time when you pass through Winterspring (stop 51).
01-第一段-1到12級.md:994｜說明｜到閃金鎮交給女牧師潔塞塔，接聖光之衣。｜Turn in to Priestess Josetta in Goldshire and accept Garments of the Light.
01-第一段-1到12級.md:999｜說明｜對受傷的衛兵施放次級治療術和真言術：韌，獎勵布甲。｜Cast Lesser Heal and Power Word: Fortitude on the wounded guards. Rewards cloth armor.
01-第一段-1到12級.md:1005｜說明｜到卡拉諾斯交給馬克薩恩·安沃爾，接聖光之衣。｜Turn in to Maxan Anvol in Kharanos and accept Garments of the Light.
01-第一段-1到12級.md:1030｜說明｜取得大地靈契，下一段在靈石處喝下才看得見大地之魂。｜Get the Earth Sapta. In the next step, drink it at the spirit stone to see the Minor Manifestation of Earth.
01-第一段-1到12級.md:1032｜說明｜喝下大地靈契後與小型大地之靈體交談。｜Drink the Earth Sapta, then talk to the Minor Manifestation of Earth.
01-第一段-1到12級.md:1010｜說明｜對受傷的登山者施放次級治療術和真言術：韌，獎勵布甲。｜Cast Lesser Heal and Power Word: Fortitude on the wounded mountaineers. Rewards cloth armor.
01-第一段-1到12級.md:1016｜說明｜到多蘭納爾交給勞爾娜·晨光，接月光之衣。｜Turn in to Laurna Morninglight in Dolanaar and accept Garments of the Moon.
01-第一段-1到12級.md:1021｜說明｜對受傷的哨兵施放次級治療術和真言術：韌，獎勵布甲。｜Cast Lesser Heal and Power Word: Fortitude on the wounded sentinels. Rewards cloth armor.
01-第一段-1到12級.md:597｜說明｜站在地脈旁使用天裔精靈的解讀地脈種族技能。｜Stand next to the leyline and use the Skyborne racial Reading the Leylines.
01-第一段-1到12級.md:843｜說明｜還不到 12 級，就在瓦拉納爾西邊打弗德飛狐首領（10 級，57.5, 71.7 一帶），練到 12 再搭船。｜If you're not level 12 yet, kill Vuldren Alphas west of Valanaar (level 10, around 57.5, 71.7) until 12, then take the boat.
01-第一段-1到12級.md:1039｜說明｜微風島職業武器支線，搭船去達拉然前完成。｜Zephras Isle class weapon quest. Finish it before taking the boat to Dalaran.
01-第一段-1到12級.md:1047｜說明｜導引可略過，已抵達瓦拉納爾就直接接 94978。｜The intro quest is optional: if you're already in Valanaar, accept 94978 directly.
01-第一段-1到12級.md:1054｜說明｜到瓦拉納爾東北方的瀑布找厄先卓斯；搭船離開微風島前做。｜Find Urs'endris at the waterfall northeast of Valanaar. Do it before you sail away from Zephras Isle.
01-第一段-1到12級.md:1054｜說明｜到瓦拉納爾東北方的瀑布找厄先卓斯｜Find Urs'endris at the waterfall northeast of Valanaar.
01-第一段-1到12級.md:1054｜說明｜搭船離開微風島前做。｜Do it before you sail away from Zephras Isle.
01-第一段-1到12級.md:1056｜說明｜厄芮德菈在申德高地巡邏，交任務學熊形態。｜Ur'endra patrols Shen'dar Highlands. Turn in to learn Bear Form.
01-第一段-1到12級.md:1059｜說明｜抵達達拉然時接；沒接也可以直接到暴風城找沙德拉斯·月樹接 94914。｜Accept it when you arrive in Dalaran. If you missed it, go straight to Sheldras Moontree in Stormwind and accept 94914.
01-第一段-1到12級.md:1059｜說明｜抵達達拉然時接｜Accept it when you arrive in Dalaran.
01-第一段-1到12級.md:1059｜說明｜沒接也可以直接到暴風城找沙德拉斯·月樹接 94914。｜If you missed it, go straight to Sheldras Moontree in Stormwind and accept 94914.
01-第一段-1到12級.md:1061｜說明｜在暴風城學傳送：月光林地，傳過去交任務。｜Learn Teleport: Moonglade in Stormwind, then teleport there to turn in.
01-第一段-1到12級.md:1076｜說明｜導引可略過，直接到鐵爐堡找穆倫·雷矛接 1678。｜The intro quest is optional: go straight to Muren Stormpike in Ironforge and accept 1678.
01-第一段-1到12級.md:1078｜說明｜維吉雷克在丹莫洛西南的冰爪洞穴附近。｜Vejrek is near the ice cave in southwestern Dun Morogh.
01-第一段-1到12級.md:1093｜說明｜在卡拉諾斯接，到丹莫洛東邊往洛克莫丹的關口找布魯格斯‧燃裔。｜Accept it in Kharanos, then find Bruegs Kindleborn at the pass from eastern Dun Morogh to Loch Modan.
01-第一段-1到12級.md:1110｜說明｜導引可略過，直接到鐵爐堡找霍夫丹·黑鬚接 2238。｜The intro quest is optional: go straight to Hulfdan Blackbeard in Ironforge and accept 2238.
01-第一段-1到12級.md:1119｜說明｜導引可略過，直接到鐵爐堡找彬克接 1880。｜The intro quest is optional: go straight to Bink in Ironforge and accept 1880.
01-第一段-1到12級.md:1134｜說明｜向瑪斯雷學傳送：月光林地，傳過去交任務。｜Learn Teleport: Moonglade from Mathrengyl Bearwalker, then teleport there to turn in.
01-第一段-1到12級.md:1141｜說明｜到奧伯丁的月夜梟獸之石使用塞納里奧月塵，擊敗月爪並與其靈魂交談；黑海岸做完再回達納蘇斯交，學熊形態。｜Use the Cenarion Moondust at the Moonkin Stone in Auberdine, defeat Lunaclaw and talk to its spirit. After finishing Darkshore, return to Darnassus to turn in and learn Bear Form.
01-第一段-1到12級.md:1141｜說明｜到奧伯丁的月夜梟獸之石使用塞納里奧月塵，擊敗月爪並與其靈魂交談｜Use the Cenarion Moondust at the Moonkin Stone in Auberdine, defeat Lunaclaw and talk to its spirit.
01-第一段-1到12級.md:1141｜說明｜黑海岸做完再回達納蘇斯交，學熊形態。｜After finishing Darkshore, return to Darnassus to turn in and learn Bear Form.
01-第一段-1到12級.md:1145｜說明｜在多蘭納爾接，到達納蘇斯交塞尤娜。｜Accept it in Dolanaar and turn it in to Syurna in Darnassus.
01-第一段-1到12級.md:1153｜說明｜在多蘭納爾接，到達納蘇斯交女祭司阿蘭希雅。｜Accept it in Dolanaar and turn it in to Priestess Alathea in Darnassus.
01-第一段-1到12級.md:1155｜說明｜完成後學星辰碎片。｜Afterwards, learn Starshards.
02-12到30級.md:721｜說明｜導引可略過，直接到暴風城找哈里·伯加德接 1639。｜The intro quest is optional: go straight to Harry Burlguard in Stormwind and accept 1639.
02-12到30級.md:725｜說明｜跟巴托比對話後他會開打，打到投降即可。｜Talk to Bartleby and he attacks you. Fight until he gives up.
02-12到30級.md:732｜說明｜蛀牙傑克在艾爾文森林東谷伐木場北方的營地。｜Dead-tooth Jack is at the camp north of Eastvale Logging Camp in Elwynn Forest.
02-12到30級.md:741｜說明｜人類在閃金鎮接的導引，可直接找加科因接 1688。｜Intro quest humans get in Goldshire. You can go straight to Gakin the Darkbinder and accept 1688.
02-12到30級.md:743｜說明｜蘇倫娜·凱爾東在艾爾文森林布瑞克威爾南瓜田的農舍。｜Surena Caledon is in the farmhouse at the Brackwell Pumpkin Patch in Elwynn Forest.
02-12到30級.md:746｜說明｜在加科因旁的召喚法陣使用符文，擊敗召喚出的虛無行者。｜Use the item at the summoning circle next to Gakin the Darkbinder and defeat the Summoned Voidwalker.
02-12到30級.md:752｜說明｜導引可略過，直接到暴風城法師區找詹妮亞·坎農接 1861。｜The intro quest is optional: go straight to Jennea Cannon in the Mage Quarter of Stormwind and accept 1861.
02-12到30級.md:760｜說明｜導引可略過，直接到暴風城軍情七處找馬迪亞斯·肖爾接 2206。｜The intro quest is optional: go straight to Master Mathias Shaw at SI:7 in Stormwind and accept 2206.
02-12到30級.md:773｜說明｜學絕望禱言。｜Learn Desperate Prayer.
02-12到30級.md:786｜說明｜交 10 塊亞麻布，不夠就去拍賣場買。｜Turn in 10 Linen Cloth. If you're short, buy some at the Auction House.
02-12到30級.md:795｜說明｜用生命符記復活艾爾文森林的亨茲·法奧克。｜Use the Symbol of Life to resurrect Henze Faulk in Elwynn Forest.
02-12到30級.md:798｜說明｜交任務學救贖。｜Turn in to learn Redemption.
02-12到30級.md:819｜說明｜獎勵月神武器。｜Rewards an Elunite weapon.
02-12到30級.md:826｜說明｜在奧伯丁的月夜梟獸之石完成身心之力後，回達納蘇斯交，學熊形態。｜After finishing Body and Heart at the Moonkin Stone in Auberdine, return to Darnassus to turn in and learn Bear Form.
02-12到30級.md:832｜說明｜在黑海岸收集毒腺，回奧伯丁交。｜Collect the venom glands in Darkshore and turn them in at Auberdine.
02-12到30級.md:835｜說明｜用解藥治療黑海岸生病的鹿。｜Use the cure on the sick deer in Darkshore.
02-12到30級.md:838｜說明｜交任務學解毒術。｜Turn in to learn Cure Poison.
02-12到30級.md:847｜說明｜在月光林地湖底找到月光之石。｜Find the Shrine Bauble at the bottom of the lake in Moonglade.
02-12到30級.md:850｜說明｜在黑海岸奧伯丁南方的海底遺跡取得月光精華。｜Get the pendant half from the underwater ruins south of Auberdine in Darkshore.
02-12到30級.md:853｜說明｜交任務學水棲形態。｜Turn in to learn Aquatic Form.
02-12到30級.md:862｜說明｜在奧瑟爾伐木場附近的箱子使用開鎖。｜Use Pick Lock on the chest near Alther's Mill.
02-12到30級.md:875｜說明｜在西部荒野克拉文之塔偷出密封信件，完成後學毒藥。｜Steal the sealed letter from Klaven's Tower in Westfall. Afterwards, learn Poisons.
02-12到30級.md:888｜說明｜學回饋。｜Learn Feedback.
02-12到30級.md:893｜說明｜暴風城接，到鐵爐堡交，學防護恐懼結界。｜Accept it in Stormwind, turn it in at Ironforge and learn Fear Ward.
02-12到30級.md:898｜說明｜暴風城接，到丹莫洛交。｜Accept it in Stormwind and turn it in at Dun Morogh.
02-12到30級.md:905｜說明｜藍色隱士酒吧在暴風城法師區。｜The Blue Recluse tavern is in the Mage Quarter of Stormwind.
02-12到30級.md:908｜說明｜交 10 塊亞麻布和 6 顆充能裂隙寶石，寶石可在拍賣場買。｜Turn in 10 Linen Cloth and 6 Charged Rift Gems. You can buy the gems at the Auction House.
02-12到30級.md:910｜說明｜獎勵法紋長袍。｜Rewards Manaweave Robe.
02-12到30級.md:921｜說明｜在梣谷擊殺薩特取得同心樹。｜Kill satyrs in Ashenvale for the Heartswood.
02-12到30級.md:925｜說明｜在加科因旁的召喚法陣使用，擊敗召喚出的魅魔。｜Use it at the summoning circle next to Gakin the Darkbinder and defeat the summoned succubus.
02-12到30級.md:938｜說明｜右鍵背包裡的勇氣之書接任務。｜Right-click Tome of Valor in your bags to start the quest.
02-12到30級.md:942｜說明｜護送達芙妮，途中會有迪菲亞伏擊。｜Escort Daphne Stilwell. Defias ambush you along the way.
02-12到30級.md:948｜說明｜要科爾寶石、白洛爾的礦石等材料。｜Needs materials such as a Kor Gem and Bailor's Ore Shipment.
02-12到30級.md:964｜說明｜瑞斯班洞穴在赤脊山西北，洞裡是巨魔。｜The Rethban Caverns are in northwestern Redridge Mountains, and they're full of ogres.
02-12到30級.md:997｜說明｜學艾露恩的賜福。｜Learn Elune's Grace.
02-12到30級.md:1053｜說明｜獎勵星結之衣。｜Rewards Astral Knot Garment.
03-30到45級.md:812｜說明｜從人形怪身上偷竊取得。｜Get it by pickpocketing humanoids.
03-30到45級.md:815｜說明｜交辛迪加徽章，可重複。｜Turn in Syndicate Emblems. Repeatable.
03-30到45級.md:934｜說明｜到塵泥沼澤交給塔貝薩。｜Turn it in to Tabetha in Dustwallow Marsh.
03-30到45級.md:870｜說明｜在斯坦哈德附近的法陣召喚並擊敗地獄獵犬。｜Summon and defeat the felhunter at the circle near Strahad Farsan.
03-30到45級.md:989｜說明｜風之召喚，到阿拉希高地找維里克‧雷鎚。｜Call of Air: find Veyric Thunderhame in Arathi Highlands.
03-30到45級.md:830｜說明｜從阿拉希高地的諾斯弗德農場偷辛迪加制服。｜Steal a Syndicate uniform from Northfold Manor in Arathi Highlands.
03-30到45級.md:951｜說明｜獎勵法師魔杖。｜Rewards a mage wand.
03-30到45級.md:924｜說明｜召喚地獄戰馬，到棘齒城交給斯坦哈德·法爾杉。｜Summon Felsteed: turn it in to Strahad Farsan in Ratchet.
03-30到45級.md:982｜說明｜導引可略過，直接找暴風城的達索瑞恩·拉爾接 1661。｜The intro quest is optional: go straight to Duthorian Rall in Stormwind and accept 1661.
03-30到45級.md:851｜說明｜在菲拉斯島的競技場連續擊敗四波對手。｜Defeat four waves of opponents in a row at the arena.
03-30到45級.md:878｜說明｜要一件奧法之袍，可請裁縫做或在拍賣場買。｜You need Robes of Arcana. Ask a tailor to make them or buy them at the Auction House.
03-30到45級.md:893｜說明｜獎勵魔化金線血袍。｜Rewards Enchanted Gold Bloodrobe.
03-30到45級.md:904｜說明｜地獄犬碎片，和 4963 二選一。｜Felhound shard. Choose either this or 4963.
03-30到45級.md:907｜說明｜地獄火碎片，和 4962 二選一。｜Infernal shard. Choose either this or 4962.
03-30到45級.md:915｜說明｜選地獄犬碎片的做這條。｜Do this one if you chose the felhound shard.
03-30到45級.md:918｜說明｜選地獄火碎片的做這條。｜Do this one if you chose the infernal shard.
03-30到45級.md:929｜說明｜交任務學召喚地獄戰馬。｜Turn in to learn Summon Felsteed.
03-30到45級.md:969｜說明｜奧達曼副本內。｜Inside the Uldaman dungeon.
04-45到60級.md:779｜說明｜擊敗塞克隆尼亞取得精華。｜Defeat Cyclonian to get its essence.
04-45到60級.md:785｜說明｜獎勵旋風武器。｜Rewards a Whirlwind weapon.
04-45到60級.md:792｜說明｜暴風城的導引可略過，直接找艾薩拉的克希雷姆接 8251。｜The intro quest is optional: go straight to Archmage Xylem in Azshara and accept 8251.
04-45到60級.md:800｜說明｜摩弗拉斯在阿塔哈卡神廟。｜Morphaz is in the Temple of Atal'Hakkar.
04-45到60級.md:808｜說明｜在暴風城接，到艾薩拉交給奧汀克。｜Accept it in Stormwind and turn it in to Ogtinc in Azshara.
04-45到60級.md:824｜說明｜在達納蘇斯（或鐵爐堡、暴風城）接，到艾薩拉交給奧汀克。｜Accept it in Darnassus (or Ironforge or Stormwind) and turn it in to Ogtinc in Azshara.
04-45到60級.md:832｜說明｜綠龍在阿塔哈卡神廟。｜The green drake is in the Temple of Atal'Hakkar.
04-45到60級.md:854｜說明｜克羅蘇斯在費伍德森林北方，要組隊。｜Kroshius is in northern Felwood. You need a group.
04-45到60級.md:889｜說明｜到西瘟疫之地寒風營地交。｜Turn it in at Chillwind Camp in Western Plaguelands.
04-45到60級.md:901｜說明｜交任務取得力量之石。｜Turn it in to get the Mightstone.
02-12到30級.md:832｜說明｜在黑海岸採地根草和月亮菇，回奧伯丁交。｜Gather Earthroot and Lunar Fungus in Darkshore, then turn them in at Auberdine.
02-12到30級.md:847｜說明｜在月神湖底找到神殿靈珠。｜Find the Shrine Bauble at the bottom of Lake Elune'ara.
02-12到30級.md:850｜說明｜在黑海岸奧伯丁南方的海底遺跡取得半塊墜飾。｜Get one half of the Pendant of the Sea Lion from the underwater ruins south of Auberdine in Darkshore.
03-30到45級.md:851｜說明｜在勇士島的競技場連續擊敗四波對手。｜Defeat four waves of opponents in a row at the arena on Fray Island.
新增.md:0｜步驟說明｜達納蘇斯這段經驗少，趕路可跳過。｜Little XP in the Darnassus section. Skip if you're in a hurry.
新增.md:0｜步驟說明｜從阿斯特蘭納往南穿過貧瘠之地，搭大升降梯下千針石林。｜Head south from Astranaar through the Barrens and take the Great Lift down to Thousand Needles.
新增.md:0｜步驟說明｜薩蘭納爾｜Thalanaar
新增.md:0｜步驟說明｜從薩蘭納爾往西穿過菲拉斯，到海邊碼頭搭船上羽月要塞。｜From Thalanaar, head west through Feralas to the dock on the coast and take the boat to Feathermoon Stronghold.
新增.md:0｜步驟說明｜有黑石深淵隊伍才在這練到 52；單人跳過。｜Level here to 52 only with a Blackrock Depths group. Skip if solo.
新增.md:0｜步驟說明｜有黑石深淵隊伍才在這練到 52｜Level here to 52 only with a Blackrock Depths group
新增.md:0｜步驟說明｜單人跳過。｜Skip if solo.
新增.md:0｜步驟說明｜打怪拾到被撕破的日記才右鍵接。｜Right-click to start only if you loot A Mangled Journal from mobs.
新增.md:0｜步驟說明｜在加德納爾 35, 59 一帶打怪。｜Kill mobs around 35, 59 in Jaedenar.
新增.md:0｜步驟說明｜在冬泉熊怪營地 32, 36 一帶打熊怪。｜Kill furbolgs around 32, 36 in the Winterfall camp.
新增.md:0｜步驟說明｜留在冬泉熊怪營地繼續打熊怪。｜Stay at the Winterfall camp and keep killing furbolgs.
新增.md:0｜步驟說明｜在達隆郡廢屋撿頭、左身、右身三個部件，右鍵組合。｜In the ruined houses of Darrowshire, collect the head, left side and right side, then right-click to combine them.
新增.md:0｜步驟說明｜還沒到 60 級就打一趟黑石深淵；還不夠再刷斯坦索姆或通靈學院。｜Not 60 yet? Run Blackrock Depths once. If that's still not enough, farm Stratholme or Scholomance.
01-第一段-1到12級.md:561｜說明｜飛行地圖沒有奧伯丁就到魯瑟蘭村碼頭搭船去奧伯丁。｜If Auberdine isn't on your flight map, take the boat from the Rut'theran Village dock to Auberdine.
01-第一段-1到12級.md:902｜說明｜飛行地圖沒有鐵爐堡就往西穿過山口回丹莫洛，直接走到卡拉諾斯。｜If Ironforge isn't on your flight map, head west through the pass back to Dun Morogh and walk straight to Kharanos.
01-第一段-1到12級.md:933｜說明｜爐石還在冷卻就從塞爾薩瑪飛鐵爐堡，再搭礦道地鐵到暴風城。｜If your hearthstone is still on cooldown, fly from Thelsamar to Ironforge, then take the Deeprun Tram to Stormwind.
01-第一段-1到12級.md:936｜說明｜飛行地圖沒有鐵爐堡就往西穿過山口回丹莫洛，沿大路走進鐵爐堡。｜If Ironforge isn't on your flight map, head west through the pass back to Dun Morogh and follow the road into Ironforge.
01-第一段-1到12級.md:885｜說明｜從暴風城出發可搭礦道地鐵到鐵爐堡。從泰達希爾提早東渡的角色先在奧伯丁搭船到暴風城，再轉地鐵。｜From Stormwind, take the Deeprun Tram to Ironforge. If you left Teldrassil early, sail from Auberdine to Stormwind first, then take the tram.
02-12到30級.md:70｜說明｜爐石沒綁暴風城或還在冷卻，就在奧伯丁碼頭搭船到暴風城。｜If your hearthstone isn't bound to Stormwind or is on cooldown, take the boat from the Auberdine dock to Stormwind.
02-12到30級.md:79｜說明｜出暴風城沿艾爾文森林大路往西，經閃金鎮過橋進西部荒野，再沿大道往南到哨兵嶺。｜Leave Stormwind and follow the Elwynn Forest road west, pass through Goldshire and cross the bridge into Westfall, then follow the main road south to Sentinel Hill.
02-12到30級.md:146｜說明｜出鐵爐堡沿大路往東，穿過東邊山口的隧道進洛克莫丹，再沿路往東北到塞爾薩瑪。｜Leave Ironforge and follow the road east, pass through the tunnel in the eastern pass into Loch Modan, then follow the road northeast to Thelsamar.
02-12到30級.md:279｜說明｜飛行地圖沒有魯瑟蘭村就和其他種族一樣搭船。｜If Rut'theran Village isn't on your flight map, take the boat like the other races.
02-12到30級.md:281｜說明｜走進魯瑟蘭村的傳送門到達納蘇斯。｜Step into the portal in Rut'theran Village to Darnassus.
02-12到30級.md:284｜說明｜走進達納蘇斯的傳送門回魯瑟蘭村。｜Step into the portal in Darnassus back to Rut'theran Village.
02-12到30級.md:300｜說明｜從暴風城沿艾爾文森林大路往東進赤脊山，順大路到湖畔鎮。｜From Stormwind, follow the Elwynn Forest road east into Redridge Mountains and stay on the road to Lakeshire.
02-12到30級.md:303｜說明｜第2站沒開湖畔鎮飛行點，就從暴風城沿艾爾文森林大路往東走到湖畔鎮。｜If you didn't unlock the Lakeshire flight point at stop 2, walk from Stormwind east along the Elwynn Forest road to Lakeshire.
02-12到30級.md:400｜說明｜從黑海岸南邊沿大路往南進梣谷，先到邁斯特拉崗哨。｜From the south of Darkshore, follow the road south into Ashenvale and go to Maestra's Post first.
02-12到30級.md:431｜說明｜這班船中途停南海鎮，到米奈希爾港再下船。｜This boat stops at Southshore on the way. Don't get off until Menethil Harbor.
02-12到30級.md:502｜說明｜已開夜色鎮飛行點就直接飛夜色鎮。沒開就飛湖畔鎮，落地後沿大路往西到赤脊山西邊的路口，再往南進暮色森林到夜色鎮。｜If you've unlocked the Darkshire flight point, fly straight to Darkshire. If not, fly to Lakeshire, then follow the road west to the junction on the west side of Redridge Mountains and head south through Duskwood to Darkshire.
03-30到45級.md:190｜說明｜在米奈希爾港北邊碼頭搭往奧伯丁的船，中途在南海鎮下船。｜Board the boat to Auberdine at the north dock in Menethil Harbor and get off at Southshore on the way.
03-30到45級.md:270｜說明｜從暮色森林西邊沿大路往南進荊棘谷，先到反抗軍營地。｜From the west side of Duskwood, follow the road south into Stranglethorn Vale and go to Rebel Camp first.
03-30到45級.md:298｜說明｜沿荊棘谷大路一路往南到藏寶海灣，經過格羅姆高附近時離部落營地遠一點。｜Follow the Stranglethorn Vale road all the way south to Booty Bay and keep well clear of the Horde camp when you pass near Grom'gol.
03-30到45級.md:331｜說明｜這班船中途停南海鎮，到奧伯丁再下船。｜This boat stops at Southshore on the way. Don't get off until Auberdine.
03-30到45級.md:377｜說明｜從魯瑟蘭村或阿斯特蘭納飛棘齒城（經塔倫迪斯營地），沿主路往南，繞開陶拉祖營地，搭大升降梯下千針石林。兩點沒開才走莫爾杉壁壘。｜Fly to Ratchet from Rut'theran Village or Astranaar (stopping at Talrendis Point), follow the main road south, steer clear of Camp Taurajo and take the Great Lift down into Thousand Needles. Take the Mor'shan Rampart route only if neither point is unlocked.
03-30到45級.md:398｜說明｜從閃光平原沿大路往南進塔納利斯，到加基森開飛行點再爐石。｜From Shimmering Flats, follow the road south into Tanaris, unlock the flight point at Gadgetzan and then hearth.
03-30到45級.md:399｜說明｜加基森｜Gadgetzan
03-30到45級.md:408｜說明｜避難谷地沒開就飛米奈希爾港，沿濕地大路往東北，過薩多爾大橋進阿拉希高地，再沿大路到避難谷地。｜If Refuge Pointe isn't unlocked, fly to Menethil Harbor, follow the road northeast through the Wetlands, cross Thandol Span into Arathi Highlands and then follow the road to Refuge Pointe.
03-30到45級.md:453｜說明｜爐石還在冷卻就從南海鎮飛鐵爐堡，搭礦道地鐵回暴風城。｜If your hearthstone is on cooldown, fly from Southshore to Ironforge and take the Deeprun Tram back to Stormwind.
03-30到45級.md:469｜說明｜在米奈希爾港南邊碼頭上船。｜Board the boat at the south dock in Menethil Harbor.
03-30到45級.md:475｜說明｜會經塞拉摩轉機。第29站沒開加基森，就從棘齒城往西南穿過貧瘠之地，搭大升降梯下千針石林，再往東到閃光平原。｜You'll connect through Theramore. If you didn't unlock Gadgetzan at stop 29, go southwest from Ratchet across the Barrens, take the Great Lift down into Thousand Needles and then head east to Shimmering Flats.
03-30到45級.md:653｜說明｜從夜色鎮往東過逆風小徑進悲傷沼澤。｜From Darkshire, head east through Deadwind Pass into the Swamp of Sorrows.
03-30到45級.md:674｜說明｜從悲傷沼澤南邊進詛咒之地，沿路往南到守望堡。｜From the south of the Swamp of Sorrows, enter the Blasted Lands and follow the road south to Nethergarde Keep.
03-30到45級.md:676｜說明｜守望堡｜Nethergarde Keep
03-30到45級.md:689｜說明｜已開尼耶爾前哨站就直接飛過去（經塞拉摩），跳過下一步。石爪峰沒開就飛阿斯特蘭納，從梣谷南邊的山洞進石爪山脈。｜If you've unlocked Nijel's Point, fly straight there (via Theramore) and skip the next step. If Stonetalon Peak isn't unlocked, fly to Astranaar and enter the Stonetalon Mountains through the cave south of Ashenvale.
03-30到45級.md:690｜說明｜從石爪峰沿大路往南下山，經過烈日石居時繞開部落營地，從石爪山脈西南邊進淒涼之地，尼耶爾前哨站在淒涼之地北邊。｜From Stonetalon Peak, follow the road south down the mountain, steer clear of the Horde camp as you pass Sun Rock Retreat, and enter Desolace from the southwest of the Stonetalon Mountains. Nijel's Point is in the north of Desolace.
03-30到45級.md:691｜說明｜尼耶爾前哨站｜Nijel's Point
03-30到45級.md:713｜說明｜爐石還在冷卻就從尼耶爾前哨站飛棘齒城（經塞拉摩），再搭船到藏寶海灣。｜If your hearthstone is on cooldown, fly from Nijel's Point to Ratchet (via Theramore), then take the boat to Booty Bay.
04-45到60級.md:72｜說明｜塞拉摩碼頭的船直達米奈希爾港。｜The boat from the Theramore dock goes straight to Menethil Harbor.
04-45到60級.md:73｜說明｜沒接調查神廟（1448）就不必去暴風城，直接從米奈希爾港飛南海鎮。｜If you didn't pick up In Search of The Temple (1448), you don't need to go to Stormwind; fly straight from Menethil Harbor to Southshore.
04-45到60級.md:83｜說明｜南海鎮沒開就飛米奈希爾港，搭北邊碼頭往奧伯丁的船，在南海鎮下船。｜If Southshore isn't unlocked, fly to Menethil Harbor, take the boat to Auberdine from the north dock and get off at Southshore.
04-45到60級.md:86｜說明｜鷹巢山｜Aerie Peak
04-45到60級.md:130｜說明｜從荒蕪之地往西走，經部落營地卡加斯旁的山路進灼熱峽谷，卡加斯要繞開。｜From the Badlands, head west and take the mountain path next to the Horde camp of Kargath into the Searing Gorge. Steer clear of Kargath.
04-45到60級.md:163｜說明｜用剛拿到的灼熱峽谷鑰匙打開巡山人貝波蒂旁的大門，穿過隧道進灼熱峽谷。｜Use the Searing Gorge key you just got to open the gate next to Mountaineer Pebblebitty and go through the tunnel into the Searing Gorge.
04-45到60級.md:211｜說明｜爐石好了就爐回塞爾薩瑪再飛；不然回瑟銀哨塔（37.90, 30.40）找飛行管理員。｜If your hearthstone is ready, hearth back to Thelsamar and fly from there; otherwise, go back to Thorium Point (37.90, 30.40) and find the flight master.
04-45到60級.md:211｜說明｜爐石好了就爐回塞爾薩瑪再飛｜If your hearthstone is ready, hearth back to Thelsamar and fly from there
04-45到60級.md:211｜說明｜不然回瑟銀哨塔（37.90, 30.40）找飛行管理員。｜Otherwise, go back to Thorium Point (37.90, 30.40) and find the flight master.
04-45到60級.md:217｜說明｜守望堡沒開就飛夜色鎮，往東過逆風小徑進悲傷沼澤，再往南進詛咒之地到守望堡。｜If Nethergarde Keep isn't unlocked, fly to Darkshire, head east through Deadwind Pass into the Swamp of Sorrows, then south into the Blasted Lands to Nethergarde Keep.
04-45到60級.md:229｜說明｜悲傷沼澤沒有聯盟飛行點：往西過逆風小徑走回夜色鎮再飛。｜The Swamp of Sorrows has no Alliance flight point: head west through Deadwind Pass back to Darkshire and fly from there.
04-45到60級.md:285｜說明｜從塔納利斯西邊的山路走坡道下安戈洛環形山，托爾瓦·尋路者就在坡道底的入口旁。｜From the mountain road west of Tanaris, take the ramp down into Un'Goro Crater. Torwa Pathfinder is right by the entrance at the bottom of the ramp.
04-45到60級.md:354｜說明｜牧師要在鐵爐堡接「塞納裏奧議會的求助」，請等爐石冷卻。其餘角色若爐石還在冷卻，可從馬紹爾營地飛奧伯丁，接往魯瑟蘭村的行程。｜Priests picking up Cenarion Aid in Ironforge should wait for their hearthstone. Otherwise, if it is on cooldown, fly from Marshal's Refuge to Auberdine and continue to Rut'theran Village.
04-45到60級.md:365｜說明｜從阿斯特蘭納沿大路往北進費伍德森林。｜From Astranaar, follow the road north into Felwood.
04-45到60級.md:392｜說明｜從這裡進木喉要塞的山洞，穿過去就是冬泉谷。｜Enter the Timbermaw Hold cave from here and pass through it to reach Winterspring.
04-45到60級.md:429｜說明｜爐石還在冷卻就從羽月要塞飛阿斯特蘭納（經奧伯丁），再往北走進費伍德。｜If your hearthstone is on cooldown, fly from Feathermoon Stronghold to Astranaar (via Auberdine), then walk north into Felwood.
04-45到60級.md:437｜說明｜回阿斯特蘭納飛刺枝林地，再往北穿過木喉要塞進冬泉谷。｜Return to Astranaar and fly to Talonbranch Glade, then head north through Timbermaw Hold into Winterspring.
04-45到60級.md:444｜說明｜會經塔倫迪斯營地轉機。｜You'll connect through Talrendis Point.
04-45到60級.md:493｜說明｜會經塞拉摩轉機。｜You'll connect through Theramore.
04-45到60級.md:512｜說明｜冷卻就走回阿斯特蘭納飛奧伯丁。｜If it's on cooldown, walk back to Astranaar and fly to Auberdine.
04-45到60級.md:528｜說明｜已開摩根的崗哨就從鐵爐堡直接飛過去。沒開就飛湖畔鎮，沿大路往北進燃燒平原，摩根的崗哨就在入口旁。｜If you've unlocked Morgan's Vigil, fly there directly from Ironforge. If not, fly to Lakeshire and follow the road north into the Burning Steppes; Morgan's Vigil is right by the entrance.
04-45到60級.md:529｜說明｜摩根的崗哨｜Morgan's Vigil
04-45到60級.md:552｜說明｜不做真正的主人（4183）就直接飛暴風城，跳過下兩步。｜If you're skipping The True Masters (4183), fly straight to Stormwind and skip the next two steps.
04-45到60級.md:916｜說明｜到西瘟疫之地冰風崗交。｜Turn it in at Chillwind Camp in the Western Plaguelands.
04-45到60級.md:573｜說明｜已開冰風崗就直接飛冰風崗，跳過下一步。飛行地圖沒有鷹巢山就飛南海鎮，照辛特蘭那次的路往東跑到鷹巢山。｜If you've unlocked Chillwind Camp, fly straight there and skip the next step. If Aerie Peak isn't on your flight map, fly to Southshore and run east to Aerie Peak the same way as on the Hinterlands trip.
04-45到60級.md:574｜說明｜從鷹巢山往北走山路進西瘟疫之地東南角（65.30, 86.80），再沿路往西到冰風崗。｜From Aerie Peak, take the mountain road north into the southeast corner of the Western Plaguelands (65.30, 86.80), then follow the road west to Chillwind Camp.
04-45到60級.md:575｜說明｜冰風崗｜Chillwind Camp
04-45到60級.md:625｜說明｜從鷹巢山往北走山路進西瘟疫之地，再往東過索多里爾河進東瘟疫之地，提里奧·弗丁就在入口旁的小屋。｜From Aerie Peak, take the mountain road north into the Western Plaguelands, then head east across the Thondroril River into the Eastern Plaguelands. Tirion Fordring is in the cabin right by the entrance.
04-45到60級.md:665｜說明｜會經冰風崗或鷹巢山轉機；飛行地圖沒有南海鎮就照舊飛暴風城，到港口搭船去奧伯丁。｜You'll connect through Chillwind Camp or Aerie Peak; if Southshore isn't on your flight map, fly to Stormwind as usual and take the boat to Auberdine from the harbor.
04-45到60級.md:665｜說明｜會經冰風崗或鷹巢山轉機｜You'll connect through Chillwind Camp or Aerie Peak
04-45到60級.md:665｜說明｜飛行地圖沒有南海鎮就照舊飛暴風城，到港口搭船去奧伯丁。｜If Southshore isn't on your flight map, fly to Stormwind as usual and take the boat to Auberdine from the harbor.
04-45到60級.md:666｜說明｜南海鎮碼頭就有往奧伯丁的船，比飛暴風城轉船快；改飛暴風城的話在暴風城港口上船。｜There's a boat to Auberdine right at the Southshore dock, which is faster than flying to Stormwind and changing boats. If you fly to Stormwind instead, board at the Stormwind harbor.
04-45到60級.md:666｜說明｜南海鎮碼頭就有往奧伯丁的船，比飛暴風城轉船快｜There's a boat to Auberdine right at the Southshore dock, which is faster than flying to Stormwind and changing boats
04-45到60級.md:666｜說明｜改飛暴風城的話在暴風城港口上船。｜If you fly to Stormwind instead, board at the Stormwind harbor.
04-45到60級.md:716｜說明｜爐石還在冷卻就走傳送門回魯瑟蘭村，飛奧伯丁，搭往米奈希爾港的船在南海鎮下船，再飛冰風崗。｜If your hearthstone is on cooldown, take the portal back to Rut'theran Village, fly to Auberdine, board the boat to Menethil Harbor, get off at Southshore and then fly to Chillwind Camp.
04-45到60級.md:717｜說明｜飛行地圖沒有冰風崗就從鷹巢山往北走山路進西瘟疫之地，再往西跑到冰風崗。｜If Chillwind Camp isn't on your flight map, take the mountain road north from Aerie Peak into the Western Plaguelands, then run west to Chillwind Camp.
04-45到60級.md:732｜說明｜安多哈爾沒有飛行點，先往南走回冰風崗再飛。｜Andorhal has no flight point. Walk back south to Chillwind Camp first and fly from there.
04-45到60級.md:765｜說明｜附近沒有飛行點：往南走回冰風崗再飛；或往東過索多里爾河，騎過東瘟疫之地到禮拜堂。｜There's no flight point nearby: walk back south to Chillwind Camp and fly from there, or head east across the Thondroril River and ride through the Eastern Plaguelands to the chapel.
04-45到60級.md:765｜說明｜附近沒有飛行點：往南走回冰風崗再飛｜There's no flight point nearby: walk back south to Chillwind Camp and fly from there
04-45到60級.md:765｜說明｜或往東過索多里爾河，騎過東瘟疫之地到禮拜堂。｜Or head east across the Thondroril River and ride through the Eastern Plaguelands to the chapel.
01-第一段-1到12級.md:546｜說明｜找永夜港的希爾瓦·菲納雯斯（1450 44.15, 45.23）搭德魯伊專用的鳥到魯瑟蘭村，再走村內傳送門進達納蘇斯。｜Find Silva Fil'naveth at Nighthaven (1450 44.15, 45.23) for the druid-only flight to Rut'theran Village, then take the portal in the village into Darnassus.
01-第一段-1到12級.md:546｜說明｜沒有這個選項就用爐石回多蘭納爾，往西走進達納蘇斯。｜If you don't have that option, hearth back to Dolanaar and walk west into Darnassus.
01-第一段-1到12級.md:1075｜說明｜在暴風城學傳送：月光林地，傳過去交任務。交完找永夜港的希爾瓦·菲納雯斯（1450 44.15, 45.23）搭德魯伊專用的鳥到魯瑟蘭村，再到碼頭搭船去奧伯丁接回主線（需遊戲實測）；或用爐石回暴風城。｜Learn Teleport: Moonglade in Stormwind, then teleport there to turn in the quest. Afterward find Silva Fil'naveth at Nighthaven (1450 44.15, 45.23) for the druid-only flight to Rut'theran Village, then take the boat from the dock to Auberdine to rejoin the main route (needs in-game testing). Or hearth back to Stormwind.
01-第一段-1到12級.md:1075｜說明｜在暴風城學傳送：月光林地，傳過去交任務。交完找永夜港的希爾瓦·菲納雯斯（1450 44.15, 45.23）搭德魯伊專用的鳥到魯瑟蘭村，再到碼頭搭船去奧伯丁接回主線（需遊戲實測）｜Learn Teleport: Moonglade in Stormwind, then teleport there to turn in the quest. Afterward find Silva Fil'naveth at Nighthaven (1450 44.15, 45.23) for the druid-only flight to Rut'theran Village, then take the boat from the dock to Auberdine to rejoin the main route (needs in-game testing)
01-第一段-1到12級.md:1075｜說明｜或用爐石回暴風城。｜Or hearth back to Stormwind.
01-第一段-1到12級.md:940｜說明｜到聖騎士訓練師布蘭度爾·鐵錘補技能。｜Visit Brandur Ironhammer, the paladin trainer, to pick up new skills.
01-第一段-1到12級.md:941｜說明｜人類術士在閃金鎮接加科因的召喚，不用這件。｜Human warlocks accept Gakin's Summons in Goldshire, so skip this one.
05-副本.md:9｜說明｜走到丹莫洛與洛克莫丹時先接進本前的任務，進本前不必專程回頭。天裔精靈不走 01-第9站：第一次到鐵爐堡時往東到丹莫洛找大地先知法森，補做法森的遠視能力、防患於未然和地底地圖再回鐵爐堡；不想繞路就略過舊鐵爐堡入侵。｜On your way through Dun Morogh and Loch Modan, pick up the pre-dungeon quests first; there's no need to backtrack just before entering. Skyborne skip 01-Stop 9: on your first visit to Ironforge, head east to Dun Morogh to find Earthseer Farsen, finish Farsen's Watch, Nip 'Em in the Bud and Underground Map, then return to Ironforge. If you'd rather not detour, skip the Old Ironforge Incursion.
02-12到30級.md:807｜說明｜從永夜港搭德魯伊專用的鳥到魯瑟蘭村，再飛奧伯丁。｜Take the druid-only flight from Nighthaven to Rut'theran Village, then fly to Auberdine.
02-12到30級.md:814｜說明｜治好 10 頭鹿後用傳送：月光林地回去交。｜After curing 10 deer, use Teleport: Moonglade to go back and turn in.
02-12到30級.md:816｜說明｜交任務學解毒術。從永夜港搭德魯伊專用的鳥到魯瑟蘭村，走村內傳送門進達納蘇斯。｜Turn in the quest to learn Cure Poison. Take the druid-only flight from Nighthaven to Rut'theran Village, then use the portal in the village to enter Darnassus.
02-12到30級.md:827｜說明｜兩個奇怪的帶鎖箱各裝半塊墜飾，一個在黑海岸奧伯丁北方的海底，一個在西部荒野西北海岸外的海底。｜The two strange locked chests each hold half of a pendant: one is on the sea floor north of Auberdine in Darkshore, the other is on the sea floor off the northwest coast of Westfall.
02-12到30級.md:828｜說明｜從永夜港搭德魯伊專用的鳥到魯瑟蘭村，飛奧伯丁，往北游到海底的帶鎖箱；再從奧伯丁搭船到暴風城，飛哨兵嶺，往西北到海岸外游下去開另一個帶鎖箱。｜Take the druid-only flight from Nighthaven to Rut'theran Village, fly to Auberdine, and swim north to the locked chest on the sea floor. Then take the boat from Auberdine to Stormwind, fly to Sentinel Hill, and head northwest to the coast and dive in to open the other locked chest.
02-12到30級.md:828｜說明｜從永夜港搭德魯伊專用的鳥到魯瑟蘭村，飛奧伯丁，往北游到海底的帶鎖箱｜Take the druid-only flight from Nighthaven to Rut'theran Village, fly to Auberdine, and swim north to the locked chest on the sea floor
02-12到30級.md:828｜說明｜再從奧伯丁搭船到暴風城，飛哨兵嶺，往西北到海岸外游下去開另一個帶鎖箱。｜Then take the boat from Auberdine to Stormwind, fly to Sentinel Hill, and head northwest to the coast and dive in to open the other locked chest.
02-12到30級.md:829｜說明｜集齊兩半後用傳送：月光林地回去，在雷姆洛斯神殿附近把墜飾合成一個。｜Once you have both halves, use Teleport: Moonglade to go back and combine the pendant halves near the Shrine of Remulos.
02-12到30級.md:832｜說明｜交任務學水棲形態。從永夜港搭德魯伊專用的鳥到魯瑟蘭村，走村內傳送門進達納蘇斯。｜Turn in the quest to learn Aquatic Form. Take the druid-only flight from Nighthaven to Rut'theran Village, then use the portal in the village to enter Darnassus.
02-12到30級.md:896｜說明｜暴風城港口搭船到奧伯丁，沿黑海岸往南進梣谷，從東邊莫爾杉壁壘進貧瘠之地往南找塔卡爾；十字路口和陶拉祖營地是部落據點，要繞開。｜Take the boat from Stormwind harbor to Auberdine, follow the Darkshore coast south into Ashenvale, then enter the Barrens from the east through Mor'shan Rampart and head south to find Takar. The Crossroads and Camp Taurajo are Horde strongholds; go around them.
02-12到30級.md:896｜說明｜暴風城港口搭船到奧伯丁，沿黑海岸往南進梣谷，從東邊莫爾杉壁壘進貧瘠之地往南找塔卡爾｜Take the boat from Stormwind harbor to Auberdine, follow the Darkshore coast south into Ashenvale, then enter the Barrens from the east through Mor'shan Rampart and head south to find Takar
02-12到30級.md:896｜說明｜十字路口和陶拉祖營地是部落據點，要繞開。｜The Crossroads and Camp Taurajo are Horde strongholds; go around them.
02-12到30級.md:901｜說明｜梣谷做完用爐石回暴風城；爐石在冷卻就往北回奧伯丁搭船到暴風城。｜After finishing Ashenvale, hearth back to Stormwind. If your Hearthstone is on cooldown, head north back to Auberdine and take the boat to Stormwind.
02-12到30級.md:901｜說明｜梣谷做完用爐石回暴風城｜After finishing Ashenvale, hearth back to Stormwind
02-12到30級.md:901｜說明｜爐石在冷卻就往北回奧伯丁搭船到暴風城。｜If your Hearthstone is on cooldown, head north back to Auberdine and take the boat to Stormwind.
05-副本.md:40｜說明｜遠程選做：入口在幽暗城上方的羅德隆廢墟，要穿過部落地區。從奧伯丁搭往米奈希爾港的船在南海鎮下船並開飛行點，往北經希爾斯布萊德丘陵、銀松森林走到提里斯法林地；出本後用爐石回暴風城，接回 02-第14站。｜Optional, far-off quest: the entrance is in the Ruins of Lordaeron above Undercity, which means crossing Horde territory. Take the Auberdine boat to Menethil Harbor, get off at Southshore and unlock its flight point, then go north through Hillsbrad Foothills and Silverpine Forest to Tirisfal Glades. After the dungeon, hearth back to Stormwind and rejoin 02-Stop 14.
02-12到30級.md:992｜說明｜從永夜港找希爾瓦·菲納雯斯搭德魯伊專用的鳥到魯瑟蘭村，走村內傳送門進達納蘇斯。｜Find Silva Fil'naveth at Nighthaven for the druid-only flight to Rut'theran Village, then use the portal in the village to enter Darnassus.
02-12到30級.md:1009｜說明｜米奈希爾港北碼頭搭船到奧伯丁（中途停南海鎮不用下船），飛阿斯特蘭納取水；回程飛奧伯丁搭船回米奈希爾港，再走回赫妲娜的洞穴。｜Take the boat from the north dock at Menethil Harbor to Auberdine (it stops at Southshore on the way; no need to get off), then fly to Astranaar to fetch the water. On the way back, fly to Auberdine, take the boat to Menethil Harbor, then walk back to Hervdana's cave.
02-12到30級.md:1009｜說明｜米奈希爾港北碼頭搭船到奧伯丁（中途停南海鎮不用下船），飛阿斯特蘭納取水｜Take the boat from the north dock at Menethil Harbor to Auberdine (it stops at Southshore on the way; no need to get off), then fly to Astranaar to fetch the water
02-12到30級.md:1009｜說明｜回程飛奧伯丁搭船回米奈希爾港，再走回赫妲娜的洞穴。｜On the way back, fly to Auberdine, take the boat to Menethil Harbor, then walk back to Hervdana's cave.
02-12到30級.md:1016｜說明｜從塞爾薩瑪飛哨兵嶺（經鐵爐堡、暴風城轉機），到遺忘的聖壇旁的池塘。｜Fly from Thelsamar to Sentinel Hill (changing flights at Ironforge and Stormwind), then go to the pond beside the Forgotten Altar.
02-12到30級.md:1019｜說明｜小型水之靈體在西部荒野池塘的聖壇附近，確切位置待實測。｜The Minor Manifestation of Water is near the altar by the Westfall pond; the exact spot still needs in-game testing.
02-12到30級.md:1021｜說明｜從哨兵嶺飛塞爾薩瑪。｜Fly from Sentinel Hill to Thelsamar.
05-副本.md:154｜說明｜要 24 級；梣谷・第一次滿 24 再接。｜Requires level 24. Ashenvale, 1st visit: accept it once you reach level 24.
05-副本.md:154｜說明｜要 24 級｜Requires level 24
05-副本.md:154｜說明｜梣谷・第一次滿 24 再接。｜Ashenvale, 1st visit: accept it once you reach level 24.
05-副本.md:155｜說明｜要 24 級；之前經過赤脊山時還不到就略過，不專程回頭。｜Requires level 24. You weren't high enough when passing through Redridge Mountains earlier, so skip it; don't backtrack.
05-副本.md:155｜說明｜之前經過赤脊山時還不到就略過，不專程回頭。｜You weren't high enough when passing through Redridge Mountains earlier, so skip it; don't backtrack.
05-副本.md:150｜說明｜走到梣谷・第一次時先接尋找凱特林（要 24 級）；赤脊山的尋找桑布雷德也要 24 級，之前經過赤脊山多半還不到。｜On reaching Ashenvale (1st visit), first pick up Seeking Caitlin (requires level 24). Songblade Search in Redridge Mountains also requires level 24, and you were most likely still too low when you passed through earlier.
05-副本.md:114｜說明｜留到 02-第19站赤脊山・第三次經過湖畔鎮再交。｜Hold it until your third pass through Lakeshire at 02-Stop 19 to turn it in.
05-副本.md:115｜說明｜留到 03-第22站暮色森林・第二次經過夜色鎮再交。｜Hold it until your second pass through Darkshire at 03-Stop 22 to turn it in.
05-副本.md:126｜說明｜要 20 級；02-第13站進達納蘇斯時還不到就之後經過再接，或略過。｜Requires level 20. If you're not high enough when you enter Darnassus at 02-Stop 13, pick it up the next time you pass through, or skip it.
05-副本.md:126｜說明｜要 20 級｜Requires level 20
05-副本.md:126｜說明｜02-第13站進達納蘇斯時還不到就之後經過再接，或略過。｜If you're not high enough when you enter Darnassus at 02-Stop 13, pick it up the next time you pass through, or skip it.
05-副本.md:127｜說明｜要 18 級，02-第13站進達納蘇斯時順接。｜Requires level 18; pick it up in passing when you enter Darnassus at 02-Stop 13.
05-副本.md:128｜說明｜要 18 級；之後經過暴風城時搭礦道地鐵到鐵爐堡接，不想專程就略過。｜Requires level 18. The next time you pass through Stormwind, take the Deeprun Tram to Ironforge to accept it, or skip it if you don't want to make a special trip.
05-副本.md:128｜說明｜要 18 級｜Requires level 18
05-副本.md:128｜說明｜之後經過暴風城時搭礦道地鐵到鐵爐堡接，不想專程就略過。｜The next time you pass through Stormwind, take the Deeprun Tram to Ironforge to accept it, or skip it if you don't want to make a special trip.
05-副本.md:121｜說明｜走到黑海岸・第二次時先接研究墮落；達納蘇斯和鐵爐堡的三件要 18–20 級，到等級經過時再接。｜On reaching Darkshore (2nd visit), first pick up Researching the Corruption. The three quests in Darnassus and Ironforge require levels 18-20; pick them up when you pass through at that level.
05-副本.md:179｜說明｜達納蘇斯接，要 24 級：從奧伯丁飛魯瑟蘭村走傳送門進城；不繞路就連同出本後的回交一起略過。｜Accept it in Darnassus, requires level 24: fly from Auberdine to Rut'theran Village and take the portal into the city. If you don't want the detour, skip it together with the turn-in after the dungeon.
05-副本.md:179｜說明｜達納蘇斯接，要 24 級：從奧伯丁飛魯瑟蘭村走傳送門進城｜Accept it in Darnassus, requires level 24: fly from Auberdine to Rut'theran Village and take the portal into the city
05-副本.md:179｜說明｜不繞路就連同出本後的回交一起略過。｜If you don't want the detour, skip it together with the turn-in after the dungeon.
05-副本.md:192｜說明｜交完回米奈希爾港，接 03-第24站的船。｜After turning in, return to Menethil Harbor and take the boat for 03-Stop 24.
03-30到45級.md:1146｜說明｜鐵爐堡飛藏寶海灣（經暴風城轉機），搭船到棘齒城。｜Fly from Ironforge to Booty Bay (changing at Stormwind), then take the boat to Ratchet.
03-30到45級.md:1148｜說明｜棘齒城搭船到藏寶海灣，飛鐵爐堡。｜Take the boat from Ratchet to Booty Bay, then fly to Ironforge.
03-30到45級.md:1150｜說明｜鐵爐堡飛南海鎮，回程飛鐵爐堡。｜Fly from Ironforge to Southshore, and fly back to Ironforge on the return.
03-30到45級.md:1153｜說明｜鐵爐堡飛米奈希爾港做濕地這段。｜Fly from Ironforge to Menethil Harbor for the Wetlands section.
03-30到45級.md:1154｜說明｜米奈希爾港南碼頭搭船到塞拉摩，開飛行點後飛棘齒城；或從米奈希爾港飛藏寶海灣（經鐵爐堡、暴風城）再搭船。｜Take the boat from the south dock at Menethil Harbor to Theramore, unlock its flight point, then fly to Ratchet. Or fly from Menethil Harbor to Booty Bay (changing at Ironforge and Stormwind) and take the boat from there.
03-30到45級.md:1154｜說明｜米奈希爾港南碼頭搭船到塞拉摩，開飛行點後飛棘齒城｜Take the boat from the south dock at Menethil Harbor to Theramore, unlock its flight point, then fly to Ratchet
03-30到45級.md:1154｜說明｜或從米奈希爾港飛藏寶海灣（經鐵爐堡、暴風城）再搭船。｜Or fly from Menethil Harbor to Booty Bay (changing at Ironforge and Stormwind) and take the boat from there.
03-30到45級.md:379｜說明｜做了達納蘇斯選做的從魯瑟蘭村飛棘齒城（經奧伯丁、塔倫迪斯營地），跳過的從阿斯特蘭納飛（經塔倫迪斯營地）；沿主路往南，繞開陶拉祖營地，搭大升降梯下千針石林。兩點沒開才走莫爾杉壁壘。｜If you did the optional Darnassus quests, fly from Rut'theran Village to Ratchet (changing at Auberdine and Talrendis Point); if you skipped them, fly from Astranaar (changing at Talrendis Point). Follow the main road south, avoid Camp Taurajo, and take the Great Lift down into Thousand Needles. Only use Mor'shan Rampart if neither point is unlocked.
03-30到45級.md:412｜說明｜交件在大河林地遠境堡的戴瑪‧布蘭特，下一站會去。｜Turn in to Demar Brant at Farholde Keep in the Riverglades; you'll go there at the next stop.
03-30到45級.md:465｜說明｜遠境堡還沒開飛行點，第一次要從湖畔鎮沿大路往東走進大河林地西口。｜Farholde Keep has no flight point yet, so on your first visit walk east along the main road from Lakeshire into the western entrance of the Riverglades.
03-30到45級.md:466｜說明｜過了赤脊山東邊的入口就是鬃丘，入口一帶的豺狼人 36–38 級，打起來痛。｜The entrance east of Redridge Mountains leads into Bristle Hills; the level 36-38 gnolls around the entrance hit hard.
03-30到45級.md:471｜說明｜史納戈是 40 級精英，打不過就跳過。｜Snargl is a level 40 elite; skip him if you can't win.
03-30到45級.md:475｜說明｜鋸刃散在豺狼人營地地上，確切點位待實測。｜The Saw Blades are scattered on the ground in the gnoll camps; the exact spots still need in-game testing.
03-30到45級.md:481｜說明｜跟著荷利去救捲捲。｜Follow Hurley to rescue Curly.
03-30到45級.md:488｜說明｜這趟先打特大螯蝦、撿新鮮肥料；泥鰭魚人頭顱留到威勒的農莊和淡水惡魔一起打。新鮮肥料點位待實測。｜On this trip, kill Jumbo Crawfish and pick up Fresh Fertilizer first. Save the Mudfin Murloc Heads for Wheeler's Grange and kill them together with the Freshwater Fiends. The Fresh Fertilizer spots still need in-game testing.
03-30到45級.md:488｜說明｜這趟先打特大螯蝦、撿新鮮肥料｜On this trip, kill Jumbo Crawfish and pick up Fresh Fertilizer first
03-30到45級.md:488｜說明｜泥鰭魚人頭顱留到威勒的農莊和淡水惡魔一起打。新鮮肥料點位待實測。｜Save the Mudfin Murloc Heads for Wheeler's Grange and kill them together with the Freshwater Fiends. The Fresh Fertilizer spots still need in-game testing.
03-30到45級.md:489｜說明｜遠境堡｜Farholde Keep
03-30到45級.md:502｜說明｜要 5 個聖潔蠟燭，材料商有賣；身上沒有就跳過這條和下一條。｜Requires 5 Holy Candles, which reagent vendors sell. If you don't have them, skip this quest and the next one.
03-30到45級.md:502｜說明｜要 5 個聖潔蠟燭，材料商有賣｜Requires 5 Holy Candles, which reagent vendors sell
03-30到45級.md:502｜說明｜身上沒有就跳過這條和下一條。｜If you don't have them, skip this quest and the next one.
03-30到45級.md:505｜說明｜墳墓在遠境堡的禮拜堂旁，點位待實測。｜The grave is beside the chapel in Farholde Keep; the spot still needs in-game testing.
03-30到45級.md:507｜說明｜乾草捆在遠境堡附近的田裡，點位待實測。｜The hay bales are in the fields near Farholde Keep; the spots still need in-game testing.
03-30到45級.md:513｜說明｜泥鰭魚人頭顱和淡水惡魔一起打。｜Farm the Mudfin Murloc Heads together with the Freshwater Fiends.
03-30到45級.md:514｜說明｜帽子在泥鰭魚人營地裡，點位待實測。｜The hat is in a Mudfin murloc camp; the spot still needs in-game testing.
03-30到45級.md:515｜說明｜在農莊獸欄四周找證據。｜Look for evidence around the farm's animal pens.
03-30到45級.md:521｜說明｜走失的馬在農莊附近，點位待實測。｜The runaway horse is near the farm; the spot still needs in-game testing.
03-30到45級.md:533｜說明｜工頭的木箱在透納的伐木場，點位待實測。｜The foreman's crate is at Turner's Logging Camp; the spot still needs in-game testing.
03-30到45級.md:536｜說明｜暮光書頁散在陽光林地，點位待實測。｜Twilight Pages are scattered across Sunnyglade; the spots still need in-game testing.
03-30到45級.md:537｜說明｜暮光怪掉戰場元帥的日誌，右鍵背包裡的日誌接任務。｜Twilight mobs drop the Field Marshal's Log; right-click the log in your bags to start the quest.
03-30到45級.md:539｜說明｜暮光指骨和拯救陽光林地打同一批暮光怪。｜Twilight Finger Bones come from the same Twilight mobs as Saving Sunnyglade.
03-30到45級.md:542｜說明｜弗索的寶藏在陽光林地往西約 6 格。｜Fuzzle's Treasure is about 6 map squares west of Sunnyglade.
03-30到45級.md:545｜說明｜在陽光林地下的洞穴對暮光之錘邪教徒和儀式法陣用水晶催化劑吸取樣本。｜In the cave beneath Sunnyglade, use the Crystal Catalyst on the Twilight's Hammer Cultists and the ritual circles to draw samples.
03-30到45級.md:549｜說明｜喬札克是 41 級精英，在洞穴深處。｜Chogg'Zac is a level 41 elite deep in the cave.
03-30到45級.md:552｜說明｜要先打完喬札克。｜You must kill Chogg'Zac first.
03-30到45級.md:554｜說明｜打暮光劫掠者收紀念物，再放到艾布林農場西邊的墳墓。｜Kill Twilight Raiders for the mementos, then place them on the graves west of Elbrim's Farm.
03-30到45級.md:559｜說明｜挑選的屍體在往泰洛堡的路邊。｜The Picked Carcass is on the roadside toward Terral's Watch.
03-30到45級.md:567｜說明｜盜獵者在羅格瑪南邊，別靠近部落的羅格瑪。｜The poachers are south of Rog'mar; stay away from Rog'mar itself, which is a Horde town.
03-30到45級.md:572｜說明｜歐巴沙是 41 級精英。｜Ombassa is a level 41 elite.
03-30到45級.md:575｜說明｜往南回遠境堡。｜Head back south to Farholde Keep.
03-30到45級.md:462｜說明｜這段沒有實跑紀錄，順序依 QuestieDB 座標排；地圖中央的羅格瑪是部落城鎮，別靠近。｜There are no run records for this stretch; the order follows QuestieDB coordinates. Rog'mar in the middle of the map is a Horde town; stay away.
03-30到45級.md:596｜說明｜新鮮的蘋果在遠境堡附近的果樹下，點位待實測。｜The Fresh Apples are under the fruit trees near Farholde Keep; the spots still need in-game testing.
03-30到45級.md:603｜說明｜從泰洛堡往西，經陽光林地南邊走到暮光之罩前的瑞夫坦中士；路過透納的伐木場順收木材。｜From Terral's Watch go west, passing south of Sunnyglade, to Sergeant Riftan in front of Twilight's Hood. Pick up the lumber as you pass Turner's Logging Camp.
03-30到45級.md:604｜說明｜木材堆在透納的伐木場，點位待實測。｜The lumber piles are at Turner's Logging Camp; the spots still need in-game testing.
03-30到45級.md:606｜說明｜任務 44 級，比這時的你高 3 級以上，怪打不動就跳過。｜This quest is level 44, 3 or more levels above you at this point; skip it if the mobs are too tough to kill.
03-30到45級.md:608｜說明｜任務 43 級，比這時的你高 3 級以上，怪打不動就跳過。｜This quest is level 43, 3 or more levels above you at this point; skip it if the mobs are too tough to kill.
03-30到45級.md:609｜說明｜任務 44 級，比這時的你高 3 級以上，怪打不動就跳過。；在暮光之罩的導引裝置召喚暮光奴隸，用靈魂石捕獲。｜This quest is level 44, 3 or more levels above you at this point; skip it if the mobs are too tough to kill. At the conduit in Twilight's Hood, summon a Twilight Thrall and capture it with a Soulstone.
03-30到45級.md:609｜說明｜在暮光之罩的導引裝置召喚暮光奴隸，用靈魂石捕獲。｜At the conduit in Twilight's Hood, summon a Twilight Thrall and capture it with a Soulstone.
03-30到45級.md:612｜說明｜任務 44 級，比這時的你高 3 級以上，怪打不動就跳過。；斥候關在暮光之罩各處，點位待實測。｜This quest is level 44, 3 or more levels above you at this point; skip it if the mobs are too tough to kill. Scouts are held prisoner all around Twilight's Hood; the spots still need in-game testing.
03-30到45級.md:612｜說明｜斥候關在暮光之罩各處，點位待實測。｜Scouts are held prisoner all around Twilight's Hood; the spots still need in-game testing.
03-30到45級.md:618｜說明｜霸主基爾加是 46 級精英。｜Overlord Kilgar is a level 46 elite.
03-30到45級.md:621｜說明｜要先打完霸主基爾加。｜You must kill Overlord Kilgar first.
03-30到45級.md:622｜說明｜往東經陽光林地、泰洛堡南邊走到火線港，繞開部落的羅格瑪；火線港沒有聯盟飛行點。｜Head east through Sunnyglade and south of Terral's Watch to Powderfuse Port, avoiding the Horde town of Rog'mar. Powderfuse Port has no Alliance flight point.
03-30到45級.md:623｜說明｜史利博在往火線港的路北邊一點。｜Sleebo is a little north of the road to Powderfuse Port.
03-30到45級.md:624｜說明｜沼澤氣體在史利博附近的噴口收，點位待實測。｜Collect the swamp gas at the vents near Sleebo; the spots still need in-game testing.
03-30到45級.md:636｜說明｜陷爪在火線港東邊的海岸。｜Trapclaw is on the coast east of Powderfuse Port.
03-30到45級.md:641｜說明｜和不友好競爭一起打東海海盜和火槍手。｜Kill the Eastsea Buccaneers and Musketeers together with Unfriendly Competition.
03-30到45級.md:650｜說明｜任務 44 級，比這時的你高 3 級以上，怪打不動就跳過。；火藥桶在風息堡廢墟的建物內。｜This quest is level 44, 3 or more levels above you at this point; skip it if the mobs are too tough to kill. The Powder Kegs are inside the buildings of the Windbreak ruins.
03-30到45級.md:650｜說明｜火藥桶在風息堡廢墟的建物內。｜The Powder Kegs are inside the buildings of the Windbreak ruins.
03-30到45級.md:655｜說明｜任務 45 級，比這時的你高 3 級以上，怪打不動就跳過。｜This quest is level 45, 3 or more levels above you at this point; skip it if the mobs are too tough to kill.
03-30到45級.md:656｜說明｜任務 44 級，比這時的你高 3 級以上，怪打不動就跳過。；在東海海盜的三艘船上各放一枚炸藥。｜This quest is level 44, 3 or more levels above you at this point; skip it if the mobs are too tough to kill. Place one explosive on each of the three Eastsea Buccaneer ships.
03-30到45級.md:656｜說明｜在東海海盜的三艘船上各放一枚炸藥。｜Place one explosive on each of the three Eastsea Buccaneer ships.
03-30到45級.md:657｜說明｜炸完船沿岸往東北送。｜After blowing up the ships, take it along the coast to the northeast to turn in.
03-30到45級.md:660｜說明｜任務 45 級，比這時的你高 3 級以上，怪打不動就跳過。；戰利品位置待實測。｜This quest is level 45, 3 or more levels above you at this point; skip it if the mobs are too tough to kill. Loot location still needs in-game testing.
03-30到45級.md:660｜說明｜戰利品位置待實測。｜Loot location still needs in-game testing.
03-30到45級.md:664｜說明｜從火線港往西南走回遠境堡。｜From Powderfuse Port, head southwest back to Farholde Keep.
03-30到45級.md:672｜說明｜會經湖畔鎮轉機。｜You'll change flights at Lakeshire.
05-副本.md:232｜說明｜右鍵背包裡的破碎的項鏈接任務。項鏈任務線要回鐵爐堡兩次，後段在出本後第二趟進本做。｜Right-click the Shattered Necklace in your bags to start the quest. The necklace questline needs two trips back to Ironforge; the later part is done on a second dungeon run after you exit.
05-副本.md:249｜說明｜要 5 塊銀錠。｜Requires 5 Silver Bars.
05-副本.md:266｜說明｜第二趟進奧達曼：從鐵爐堡飛塞爾薩瑪，往東南走進荒蕪之地，從北邊入口進本。｜Second Uldaman run: fly from Ironforge to Thelsamar, head southeast into the Badlands, and enter the dungeon from the north entrance.
05-副本.md:266｜說明｜項鏈任務線後段；隊伍不想再進本就略過到最後。｜Later part of the necklace questline. If your group doesn't want another run, skip it to the end.
05-副本.md:266｜說明｜項鏈任務線後段｜Later part of the necklace questline
05-副本.md:266｜說明｜隊伍不想再進本就略過到最後。｜If your group doesn't want another run, skip it to the end.
05-副本.md:271｜說明｜能量源在尾王阿札達斯身上。｜The power source is on the final boss, Archaedas.
05-副本.md:272｜說明｜出本走回塞爾薩瑪飛鐵爐堡。｜After the dungeon, walk back to Thelsamar and fly to Ironforge.
03-30到45級.md:1167｜說明｜棘齒城搭船到藏寶海灣。｜Take the boat from Ratchet to Booty Bay.
03-30到45級.md:1169｜說明｜藏寶海灣搭船回棘齒城。｜Take the boat from Booty Bay back to Ratchet.
03-30到45級.md:1176｜說明｜阿拉希這段：棘齒城搭船到藏寶海灣，飛避難谷地（經暴風城、鐵爐堡轉機）；回程反向。｜Arathi leg: take the boat from Ratchet to Booty Bay, then fly to Refuge Pointe (changing at Stormwind and Ironforge). Reverse the trip on the way back.
03-30到45級.md:1176｜說明｜阿拉希這段：棘齒城搭船到藏寶海灣，飛避難谷地（經暴風城、鐵爐堡轉機）｜Arathi leg: take the boat from Ratchet to Booty Bay, then fly to Refuge Pointe (changing at Stormwind and Ironforge)
03-30到45級.md:1176｜說明｜回程反向。｜Reverse the trip on the way back.
04-45到60級.md:798｜說明｜避難谷地飛米奈希爾港，南碼頭搭船到塞拉摩，飛薩蘭納爾，往東走進千針石林。｜Fly from Refuge Pointe to Menethil Harbor, take the boat from the south dock to Theramore, fly to Thalanaar, then walk east into Thousand Needles.
04-45到60級.md:799｜說明｜薩蘭納爾飛塞拉摩，搭船到米奈希爾港，飛避難谷地。｜Fly from Thalanaar to Theramore, take the boat to Menethil Harbor, then fly to Refuge Pointe.
04-45到60級.md:134｜說明｜瑪爾戈的角開灼熱峽谷鑰匙任務線；不打就跳過第 12、31–32、34、36、39–41 步，第 42 步從鐵爐堡飛回瑟銀哨塔。｜Margol's Horn starts the Searing Gorge key questline. If you skip it, skip steps 12, 31-32, 34, 36 and 39-41, and for step 42 fly back to Thorium Point from Ironforge.
04-45到60級.md:134｜說明｜瑪爾戈的角開灼熱峽谷鑰匙任務線｜Margol's Horn starts the Searing Gorge key questline
04-45到60級.md:134｜說明｜不打就跳過第 12、31–32、34、36、39–41 步，第 42 步從鐵爐堡飛回瑟銀哨塔。｜If you skip it, skip steps 12, 31-32, 34, 36 and 39-41, and for step 42 fly back to Thorium Point from Ironforge.
04-45到60級.md:162｜說明｜有館長的證明（3201）才回貝波蒂拿鑰匙；沒有就跳到第 42 步。｜Only go back to Mountaineer Pebblebitty for the key if you have At Last! (3201). If you don't, skip to step 42.
04-45到60級.md:162｜說明｜有館長的證明（3201）才回貝波蒂拿鑰匙｜Only go back to Mountaineer Pebblebitty for the key if you have At Last! (3201)
04-45到60級.md:162｜說明｜沒有就跳到第 42 步。｜If you don't, skip to step 42.
04-45到60級.md:165｜說明｜沒有灼熱峽谷鑰匙的人從鐵爐堡直飛回灼熱峽谷；已經穿隧道進來的跳過。｜If you don't have the Searing Gorge key, fly straight back to Searing Gorge from Ironforge. If you already came in through the tunnel, skip this.
04-45到60級.md:165｜說明｜沒有灼熱峽谷鑰匙的人從鐵爐堡直飛回灼熱峽谷｜If you don't have the Searing Gorge key, fly straight back to Searing Gorge from Ironforge
04-45到60級.md:165｜說明｜已經穿隧道進來的跳過。｜If you already came in through the tunnel, skip this.
04-45到60級.md:263｜說明｜卷軸在塵泥沼澤西南的迅猛龍洞穴，先往東走回塞拉摩（1445 67.48, 51.30）再飛。｜The scroll is in the raptor cave southwest of Dustwallow Marsh. Walk back east to Theramore (1445 67.48, 51.30) first, then fly.
04-45到60級.md:821｜說明｜暴風城的導引可略過；到艾薩拉仍要先找薩納斯傳送上懸崖。｜You can skip the Stormwind introduction. You still need to find Sanath Lim-yo first to be teleported up the cliff when you reach Azshara.
04-45到60級.md:821｜說明｜暴風城的導引可略過｜You can skip the Stormwind introduction
04-45到60級.md:821｜說明｜到艾薩拉仍要先找薩納斯傳送上懸崖。｜You still need to find Sanath Lim-yo first to be teleported up the cliff when you reach Azshara.
04-45到60級.md:822｜說明｜有接導引才在這裡交。｜Turn it in here only if you picked up the introduction.
04-45到60級.md:823｜說明｜找薩納斯對話，請他把你傳送上懸崖找大法師克希雷姆。｜Talk to Sanath Lim-yo and ask him to teleport you up the cliff to find Archmage Xylem.
04-45到60級.md:826｜說明｜塵埃在山下，回去同樣找薩納斯傳送上來。｜The dust is at the foot of the mountain; go back to Sanath Lim-yo the same way to be teleported up.
04-45到60級.md:832｜說明｜摩弗拉斯在阿塔哈卡神廟。下懸崖用爐石，或找克希雷姆身邊的傳送選項（需遊戲實測）。｜Morphaz is in the Temple of Atal'Hakkar. To get down the cliff, use your Hearthstone or look for the teleport option next to Xylem (needs in-game testing).
04-45到60級.md:492｜說明｜托爾瓦旁沒有飛行點：從他身後的坡道出安戈洛環形山進塔納利斯，往東北跑到加基森。｜There's no flight point near Torwa: leave Un'Goro Crater by the ramp behind him into Tanaris and run northeast to Gadgetzan.
04-45到60級.md:499｜說明｜在奧伯丁旅店找旅店老闆莎希因綁爐石（37.04, 44.13）。｜Set your Hearthstone with Innkeeper Shaussiy at the Auberdine inn (37.04, 44.13).
04-45到60級.md:683｜說明｜從死木蒸鍋往南走到刺枝林地飛行點（1448 62.49, 24.24）起飛，會經奧伯丁轉機。｜From the Deadwood Cauldron, head south to the Talonbranch Glade flight point (1448 62.49, 24.24) and take off from there; you'll change flights at Auberdine.
04-45到60級.md:777｜說明｜身上有真正的主人（4186）才去湖畔鎮；沒有就跳過第 60–62 步，從暴風城直飛摩根的崗哨。｜Only go to Lakeshire if you have The True Masters (4186). If you don't, skip steps 60-62 and fly straight from Stormwind to Morgan's Vigil.
04-45到60級.md:777｜說明｜身上有真正的主人（4186）才去湖畔鎮｜Only go to Lakeshire if you have The True Masters (4186)
04-45到60級.md:777｜說明｜沒有就跳過第 60–62 步，從暴風城直飛摩根的崗哨。｜If you don't, skip steps 60-62 and fly straight from Stormwind to Morgan's Vigil.
05-副本.md:351｜說明｜骨架待補：只列入口和進本前的愛與家庭（5848），找畫、出本回交和回主線交通還沒寫。｜Skeleton only for now: it lists just the entrance and Of Love and Family (5848) before the dungeon; finding the painting, the post-dungeon turn-in and the travel back to the main route aren't written yet.
05-副本.md:323｜說明｜前置是索瑞森廢墟（3701），04-第57站在鐵爐堡交完再接。｜The prerequisite is The Smoldering Ruins of Thaurissan (3701); accept it after you turn that in at Ironforge at 04-Stop 57.
05-副本.md:324｜說明｜在詛咒之地接到才交。｜Turn it in only once you've picked it up in the Blasted Lands.
05-副本.md:325｜說明｜卡拉諾斯不順路，不繞就略過。｜Kharanos is out of the way; skip it unless you want to make the detour.
05-副本.md:319｜說明｜在鐵爐堡交完索瑞森廢墟就接卡蘭·巨錘；燃燒平原的幾件在摩根的崗哨一帶接。｜Pick up Kharan Mighthammer once you've turned in The Smoldering Ruins of Thaurissan at Ironforge. The Burning Steppes quests are accepted around Morgan's Vigil.
06-路線外任務.md:17｜說明｜塔納利斯・第一次（第40站）經過時，有空再接。｜Pick this up if you have time when you pass through Tanaris (1st visit, stop 40).
06-路線外任務.md:20｜說明｜塔納利斯・第三次（第47站）經過時，有空再接。｜Pick this up if you have time when you pass through Tanaris (3rd visit, stop 47).
06-路線外任務.md:25｜說明｜大河林地兩站沒排（精英、副本、要特殊材料或太偏），跳過不影響練級。｜The two Riverglades stops weren't scheduled (elites, dungeons, special materials or too far out); skipping them doesn't affect leveling.
06-路線外任務.md:162｜說明｜費伍德森林・第一次（第49站）經過時，有空再接。｜Pick this up if you have time when you pass through Felwood (1st visit, stop 49).
06-路線外任務.md:586｜說明｜安戈洛環形山・第二次（第52站）經過時，有空再接。｜Pick this up if you have time when you pass through Un'Goro Crater (2nd visit, stop 52).
06-路線外任務.md:808｜說明｜西瘟疫之地・第一次（第55站）經過時，有空再接。｜Pick this up if you have time when you pass through Western Plaguelands (1st visit, stop 55).

06-路線外任務.md:972｜說明｜灼熱峽谷（第45站）經過時，有空再接。｜Pick this up if you have time when you pass through Searing Gorge (stop 45).
06-路線外任務.md:973｜說明｜荊棘谷・第四次（第46站）經過時，有空再接。｜Pick this up if you have time when you pass through Stranglethorn Vale (4th visit, stop 46).

06-路線外任務.md:1504｜說明｜荒蕪之地（第36站）經過時，有空再接。｜Pick this up if you have time when you pass through Badlands (stop 36).
06-路線外任務.md:1652｜說明｜淒涼之地（第38站）經過時，有空再接。｜Pick this up if you have time when you pass through Desolace (stop 38).
